================================================================================
  BLUEPRINT ARSITEKTUR & ATURAN BISNIS: POLARIS AI GENERATION & VISUAL ENGINE
================================================================================

1. FILOSOFI & LATAR BELAKANG BISNIS:
------------------------------------
A. Masalah Fundamental Generator Gambar AI Konvensional:
   - Model generatif (DALL-E 3 / Midjourney / Flux) TIDAK memiliki layout deterministik.
   - AI menghasilkan teks cacat, typo, huruf terbalik, dan bahasa alien.
   - Wajah anggota dewan jika digambar oleh AI menghasilkan deformasi (kartunis/deepfake),
     yang berisiko fatal terhadap reputasi politik dan melanggar etika kampanye.
   - Logo partai politik (Golkar, PDI-P, Gerindra, dll) atau lembaga (DPR/DPRD) selalu
     terdistorsi dan melanggar pakem resmi lambang negara/partai jika digenerate AI.

B. Paradigma Solusi: 3-Layer Composite Architecture:
   - LAYER 1 [Dasar / AI-Generated Canvas]:
     AI hanya bertugas menghasilkan karya fotografi dokumenter 35mm / risograph
     yang realistis dan humanis, TANPA TEKS, TANPA LOGO, dan dengan zona ruang
     kosong (negative space) yang terkontrol.
   - LAYER 2 [Tengah / Subjek Asli Tokoh]:
     Foto fisik asli anggota dewan dari Katalog Foto Profil (PNG transparan)
     ditempelkan ke kanvas dengan bayangan kontak alami (ambient drop-shadow) dan
     penyamaan pencahayaan (tone matching). Wajah 100% otentik.
   - LAYER 3 [Atas / Tipografi & Data Deterministik]:
     Seluruh teks judul (Bebas Neue / Anton), kutipan pernyataan, nama dewan,
     gelar, dan barcode editorial dirender langsung oleh kode program via SVG.
     100% tajam, pixel-perfect, dan bebas halusinasi.
   - LAYER 4 [Paling Depan / Logo Resmi Terkunci]:
     Logo partai dan logo lembaga (DPR RI/DPRD) ditempatkan di sudut aman
     (corner-locked) dari file SVG/PNG resmi dan dilarang keras digenerate AI.

2. METODOLOGI "ATM" (AMATI, TIRU, MODIFIKASI) & PRE-ANALYZED BLUEPRINT:
------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Sistem tidak menjiplak gambar referensi atau menempelkan overlay di atasnya.
- Sistem membedah seluruh referensi di `uploads/references/poster/` menjadi 8 Arketipe Modular:
  1. STREET_JOURNALISM      : Montase koran 3-kolom isu kerakyatan, pasar, duotone merah-hitam (POSTER PKL).
  2. POP_OUT_HERO          : Subjek menembus bingkai luar, judul interlocking di belakang bahu (THE EDGE).
  3. STATEMENT_PROFILE     : Potret karismatik dominan, kartu glassmorphism, kutipan visi (STEVE JOBS / B&W).
  4. RETRO_CAMPAIGN        : Format edukasi publik vintage 70s-80s dengan badge warna kontras (Poster Acara).
  5. TORN_PAPER_NEWS       : Broadsheet koran sobek tengah, 3 lingkaran data stat, 2 thumbnail crop (Techno Veritas / Malam Jago Merah).
  6. RETRO_SPLIT_MITIGATION: Infografis retro keselamatan publik, circular magnifier inset, split 2-kolom mitigasi (EARTHQUAKE INFOGRAFIS).
  7. MODULAR_GRID_CARDS    : 5 kartu framework analitis bernomor 01-05 dengan aksen blok merah dan multi-crop B&W (5 Modern Font Pairings).
  8. VINTAGE_PERSONA_GAZETTE: Broadsheet klasik koran sejarah krem, narasi panjang, dan multi-crop foto dokumentasi (The S News).

3. SINGLE-PASS MULTI-CROP STRATEGY (1x CALL AI IMAGE -> BANYAK SLOT GAMBAR):
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Untuk poster kompleks dengan 2-5 sub-foto (misal thumbnail bawah di Malam Jago Merah atau 5 kartu di Modern Font),
  sistem TIDAK PERLU memanggil DALL-E berkali-kali.
- Sharp `MultiRegionCropper.extractSubRegions()` memotong koordinat spesifik dari 1 kanvas AI dasar (atau mengambil
  dari `tenantMembers.photoGallery` jika tersedia) secara lokal (< 50ms, Rp 0) dan menyuntikkannya ke tag `<img>` Satori.

4. RESEARCH & EVIDENCE ENGINE (GROUNDING KEBIJAKAN):
----------------------------------------------------
- Menghubungkan regulasi internal, berita media lokal, Crossref DOI, dan web discovery.
- Angka statistik dan pasal hukum hanya boleh diekstrak dari sumber terverifikasi.
- Diagram dan poin kebijakan dirender secara deterministik oleh sistem.

DATABASE SCHEMA & PERSISTENCE (Drizzle ORM) (5 Berkas)\n================================================================================\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/database/src/schema/identity.ts (102 baris, 5.4 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport { pgTable, uuid, varchar, timestamp, text, boolean, integer, jsonb } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import { legislativeLevelEnum } from './enums.js';

export const electoralDistricts = pgTable('electoral_districts', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  dapilCode: varchar('dapil_code', { length: 50 }).notNull().unique(),
  dapilName: varchar('dapil_name', { length: 100 }).notNull(),
  provinceName: varchar('province_name', { length: 100 }).notNull(),
  regencyCoverage: text('regency_coverage').array().notNull(),
  totalVoters: integer('total_voters'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const tenantMembers = pgTable('tenant_members', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  email: varchar('email', { length: 255 }).notNull().unique(),
  passwordHash: varchar('password_hash', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  fullName: varchar('full_name', { length: 150 }).notNull(),
  phoneNumber: varchar('phone_number', { length: 30 }).notNull(),
  partyAffiliation: varchar('party_affiliation', { length: 100 }),
  legislativeLevel: legislativeLevelEnum('legislative_level').notNull(),
  electoralDistrictId: uuid('electoral_district_id').references(() => electoralDistricts.id, { onDelete: 'restrict' }),
  customDapilName: varchar('custom_dapil_name', { length: 100 }),
  personalCoverage: text('personal_coverage').array().default([]),
  commissionId: uuid('commission_id'),
  commissionName: varchar('commission_name', { length: 150 }),
  photoUrl: text('photo_url'),
  photoGallery: text('photo_gallery').array().default([]),
  gender: varchar('gender', { length: 20 }),
  birthDate: varchar('birth_date', { length: 30 }),
  education: text('education'),
  courses: text('courses').array(),
  issueInterests: text('issue_interests').array(),
  isVerified: boolean('is_verified').default(false).notNull(),
  accountStatus: varchar('account_status', { length: 30 }).default('ACTIVE').notNull(),
  username: varchar('username', { length: 50 }).unique(),
  mustChangePassword: boolean('must_change_password').default(false).notNull(),
  temporaryPasswordPlaintextPreview: varchar('temporary_password_plaintext_preview', { length: 100 }),
  passwordChangedAt: timestamp('password_changed_at', { withTimezone: true }),
  internalNotes: text('internal_notes'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const emailOtps = pgTable('email_otps', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  email: varchar('email', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  otpCode: varchar('otp_code', { length: 10 }).notNull(),

export const tenantActivityLogs = pgTable('tenant_activity_logs', {

import { tenantMembers } from './identity.js';
import { contentStatusEnum, assetTypeEnum } from './enums.js';

export const contentPublications = pgTable('content_publications', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  tenantId: uuid('tenant_id').notNull().references(() => tenantMembers.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  title: varchar('title', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  slug: varchar('slug', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  excerpt: text('excerpt').notNull(),
  bodyContentMarkdown: text('body_content_markdown').notNull(),
  wordCount: integer('word_count').notNull(),
  status: contentStatusEnum('status').default('DRAFT').notNull(),
  canonicalUrl: text('canonical_url').notNull(),
  commentCount: integer('comment_count').default(0).notNull(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id'),
  publishedAt: timestamp('published_at', { withTimezone: true }),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({

  unqTenantSlug: unique().on(t.tenantId, t.slug),
}));

export const mediaAssets = pgTable('media_assets', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  publicationId: uuid('publication_id').notNull().references(() => contentPublications.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  assetType: assetTypeEnum('asset_type').notNull(),
  r2StorageUrl: text('r2_storage_url').notNull(),
  cdnPublicUrl: text('cdn_public_url').notNull(),
  promptUsed: text('prompt_used'),
  mimeType: varchar('mime_type', { length: 50 }).default('image/webp').notNull(),
  fileSizeBytes: bigint('file_size_bytes', { mode: 'number' }).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const socialSyndicationPacks = pgTable('social_syndication_packs', {

});

  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),

\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/database/src/schema/research.ts (180 baris, 8.4 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport { pgTable, uuid, varchar, text, integer, real, timestamp, boolean, jsonb, index, check } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core';
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { tenantMembers } from './identity.js';
import {
  researchJobStatusEnum,
  questionIntentEnum,
  questionStatusEnum,
  sourceTypeEnum,
  publisherTypeEnum,
  sourceLifecycleEnum,
  claimStatusEnum,
  supportTypeEnum,
} from './enums.js';

/**
 * 1. RESEARCH JOBS: Sesi / Pekerjaan Riset Otonom POLARIS
 */
export const researchJobs = pgTable('research_jobs', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  tenantId: uuid('tenant_id').notNull().references(() => tenantMembers.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  topic: varchar('topic', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  userPrompt: text('user_prompt').notNull(),
  targetRegion: varchar('target_region', { length: 100 }).notNull(),
  status: researchJobStatusEnum('status').default('PENDING').notNull(),
  evidenceSufficient: boolean('evidence_sufficient').default(false).notNull(),
  qcPassed: boolean('qc_passed').default(false).notNull(),
  summaryReport: text('summary_report'),
  metadata: jsonb('metadata').default(sql`'{}'::jsonb`),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp('updated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  tenantStatusIdx: index('idx_research_jobs_tenant_status').on(t.tenantId, t.status),
}));

/**
 * 2. RESEARCH QUESTIONS: Pertanyaan Riset Berstruktur (Causal / Factual Requirements)
 */
export const researchQuestions = pgTable('research_questions', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id').notNull().references(() => researchJobs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  question: text('question').notNull(),
  intentCategory: questionIntentEnum('intent_category').default('POLICY_EVALUATION').notNull(),
  temporalBound: varchar('temporal_bound', { length: 50 }),
  geographicScope: varchar('geographic_scope', { length: 100 }),
  minRequiredEvidence: integer('min_required_evidence').default(1).notNull(),
  status: questionStatusEnum('status').default('UNRESOLVED').notNull(),
  resolutionSummary: text('resolution_summary'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  jobIdx: index('idx_research_questions_job').on(t.researchJobId),
}));

/**
 * 3. SOURCES: Registri Sumber Universal & Status Siklus Hidup
 */
export const sources = pgTable('sources', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  url: text('url').notNull(),
  canonicalUrl: text('canonical_url'),
  sourceType: sourceTypeEnum('source_type').notNull(),
  publisherName: varchar('publisher_name', { length: 255 }),
  publisherType: publisherTypeEnum('publisher_type').default('MEDIA').notNull(),
  publisherCredibilityScore: real('publisher_credibility_score').default(0.5).notNull(),
  doi: varchar('doi', { length: 100 }),
  isPeerReviewed: boolean('is_peer_reviewed'),
  hasRetractionsOrCorrections: boolean('has_retractions_or_corrections').default(false).notNull(),
  lifecycleStatus: sourceLifecycleEnum('lifecycle_status').default('DISCOVERED').notNull(),
  rejectionReason: text('rejection_reason'),
  retrievedAt: timestamp('retrieved_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  urlIdx: index('idx_sources_url').on(t.url),
  lifecycleIdx: index('idx_sources_lifecycle').on(t.lifecycleStatus),
}));

/**
 * 4. RESEARCH JOB SOURCES: Relasi Banyak-ke-Banyak Antara Job dan Sumber
 */
export const researchJobSources = pgTable('research_job_sources', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id').notNull().references(() => researchJobs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  sourceId: uuid('source_id').notNull().references(() => sources.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  relevanceScore: real('relevance_score').default(0.5).notNull(),
  queryUsed: text('query_used'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  jobSourceIdx: index('idx_research_job_sources_pair').on(t.researchJobId, t.sourceId),
}));

/**
 * 5. SOURCE VERSIONS: Snapshot Isi Sumber & Bukti Audit Trail
 */
export const sourceVersions = pgTable('source_versions', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  sourceId: uuid('source_id').notNull().references(() => sources.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  contentHash: varchar('content_hash', { length: 64 }).notNull(),
  title: text('title').notNull(),
  rawContent: text('raw_content'),
  extractedText: text('extracted_text').notNull(),
  httpStatus: integer('http_status').default(200).notNull(),
  fetchedAt: timestamp('fetched_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  sourceHashIdx: index('idx_source_versions_source_hash').on(t.sourceId, t.contentHash),
}));

/**
 * 6. SOURCE ASSESSMENTS: Riwayat Penilaian Relevansi & Kredibilitas
 */
export const sourceAssessments = pgTable('source_assessments', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  sourceId: uuid('source_id').notNull().references(() => sources.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  substantiveScore: real('substantive_score').notNull(),
  methodologyScore: real('methodology_score'),
  recencyScore: real('recency_score').notNull(),
  overallScore: real('overall_score').notNull(),
  assessmentReason: text('assessment_reason').notNull(),
  isAccepted: boolean('is_accepted').notNull(),
  evaluatedAt: timestamp('evaluated_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

/**
 * 7. EVIDENCE SPANS: Petikan Kutipan & Bukti Faktual Granular
 */
export const evidenceSpans = pgTable('evidence_spans', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  sourceId: uuid('source_id').notNull().references(() => sources.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  sourceVersionId: uuid('source_version_id').references(() => sourceVersions.id, { onDelete: 'set null' }),
  verbatimQuote: text('verbatim_quote').notNull(),
  contextSnippet: text('context_snippet').notNull(),
  sectionOrPage: varchar('section_or_page', { length: 100 }),
  confidenceScore: real('confidence_score').default(1.0).notNull(),
  isVerified: boolean('is_verified').default(true).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  sourceIdx: index('idx_evidence_spans_source').on(t.sourceId),
}));

/**
 * 8. CLAIMS: Klaim Faktual / Argumen Kebijakan yang Diselidiki
 */
export const claims = pgTable('claims', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id').notNull().references(() => researchJobs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  researchQuestionId: uuid('research_question_id').references(() => researchQuestions.id, { onDelete: 'set null' }),
  claimStatement: text('claim_statement').notNull(),
  status: claimStatusEnum('status').default('UNSUPPORTED').notNull(),
  confidenceScore: real('confidence_score').default(0.0).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  jobClaimIdx: index('idx_claims_job').on(t.researchJobId),
}));

/**
 * 9. CLAIM EVIDENCE: Pemetaan Relasi Klaim dengan Bukti
 */
export const claimEvidence = pgTable('claim_evidence', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  claimId: uuid('claim_id').notNull().references(() => claims.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  evidenceSpanId: uuid('evidence_span_id').notNull().references(() => evidenceSpans.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  supportType: supportTypeEnum('support_type').default('SUPPORTS').notNull(),
  relationshipExplanation: text('relationship_explanation'),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
}, (t) => ({
  claimEvidencePairIdx: index('idx_claim_evidence_pair').on(t.claimId, t.evidenceSpanId),
}));

/**
 * 10. RESEARCH OUTPUTS: Hasil Naskah Riset & Bibliografi Lengkap
 */
export const researchOutputs = pgTable('research_outputs', {
  id: uuid('id').defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  researchJobId: uuid('research_job_id').notNull().references(() => researchJobs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  title: varchar('title', { length: 255 }).notNull(),
  executiveSummary: text('executive_summary').notNull(),
  markdownBody: text('markdown_body').notNull(),
  bibliographyJson: jsonb('bibliography_json').notNull(),
  qcReport: jsonb('qc_report').notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp('created_at', { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

\n\n================================================================================\n# BAGIAN: 2. CORE AI ENGINE & CREATIVE POSTER ARCHITECTURE (10 Berkas)\n================================================================================\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/types.ts (183 baris, 7.2 KB) ---\n======================================================================\n/**
 * POLARIS Creative Poster Engine v2.0 - Multi-Block Collage Contracts
 * Mendefinisikan tipe data kontrak untuk arketipe poster,
 * slot koordinat deterministik, multi-block editorial grid, dan prompt compiler.
 */

export type CreativePosterArchetype =
  | 'STREET_JOURNALISM'       // Inspirasi: "POSTER PKL", "OKE GAS" (Duotone, Risograph, Isu Kerakyatan)
  | 'POP_OUT_HERO'            // Inspirasi: "THE EDGE", "Fly High", "Polaroid" (Subjek Menembus Frame, Modern Bold)
  | 'STATEMENT_PROFILE'       // Inspirasi: "Steve Jobs", "Modern Typography" (Potret Karismatik, Glassmorphic, Kutipan Tegas)
  | 'RETRO_CAMPAIGN'          // Inspirasi: "Poster Acara" (Vintage 70s-80s, Badge, Edukasi Publik)
  | 'TORN_PAPER_NEWS'         // Inspirasi: "Techno Veritas" / "Night of Tremors" / "Malam Jago Merah" (Broadsheet, Torn Centerfold, Circular Badges)
  | 'RETRO_SPLIT_MITIGATION'  // Inspirasi: "EARTHQUAKE INFOGRAFIS RETRO STYLE" / Get To Know Earthquake (Split 2-kolom, Kaca Pembesar, Ikon Mitigasi)
  | 'MODULAR_GRID_CARDS'      // Inspirasi: "5 Modern Font Pairings" (5 Kartu Bernomor 01-05, Aksen Kotak Merah, Multi-Crop)
  | 'VINTAGE_PERSONA_GAZETTE'; // Inspirasi: "The S News" / "Biografi Singkat Steve Jobs" (Koran Antik, Multi-Photo Subjek, Narasi Panjang)

export type PosterAspectRatio = '4:5' | '9:16' | '1:1' | '3:4' | '16:9';

export type SubjectCompositionMode =
  | 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY'      // Seperti Poster PKL/Sampah: Semua subjek foto adalah ilustrasi masyarakat, nama Dewan di Header/Footer
  | 'FIGURE_CENTERPIECE';  // Foto PNG asli Anggota Dewan masuk ke dalam kolase (di-filter sesuai tema)

export interface PosterSlotCoordinate {
  x: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0 (Persentase dari lebar kanvas)
  y: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0 (Persentase dari tinggi kanvas)
  w: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0
  h?: number; // 0.0 s/d 1.0
  anchor?: 'top-left' | 'top-right' | 'bottom-left' | 'bottom-right' | 'center';
}

/**
 * Definisi Slot Teks Multi-Blok (Untuk layout koran/infografis kompleks)
 */
export interface EditorialTextBlockSpec {
  slotId: string; // e.g., 'left_positive', 'left_negative_box', 'center_giant_title', 'center_story', 'right_cause', 'bottom_banner'
  role: 'COLUMN_SECTION' | 'SOLID_COLOR_BOX' | 'GIANT_STACKED_TITLE' | 'BOTTOM_FULL_BANNER';
  coordinate: PosterSlotCoordinate;
  typography: {
    headerFont: 'Anton' | 'Bebas Neue' | 'Oswald';
    headerSizePx: number;
    headerColor: string;
    bodyFont: 'Plus Jakarta Sans' | 'Space Mono' | 'Oswald';
    bodySizePx: number;
    bodyColor: string;
    boxBgColor?: string;
    textAlign: 'left' | 'justify' | 'center';
  };
  constraints: {
    headerMaxChars: number;
    bodyMinChars: number;
    bodyMaxChars: number;
  };
  defaultReferenceHeader: string;
}

/**
 * Aturan Substitusi Subjek Visual 1:1 (Amati, Tiru, Modifikasi)
 */
export interface VisualCutoutSlotSpec {
  slotId: string; // e.g., 'upper_right_cutout', 'mid_left_hero_cutout', 'bottom_street_scene'
  spatialZonePrompt: string;
  originalReferenceSubject: string;
  photoTreatment: 'BW_HALFTONE_CUTOUT' | 'DUOTONE_RED_BLACK' | 'VINTAGE_WARM';
}

export interface SynthesizedEditorialCopy {
  mainBannerTitle: string;
  mainBannerSubheader: string;
  mainBannerDefinitionBody: string;
  blocks: Record<
    string,
    {
      header: string;
      body: string;
    }
  >;
  mappedVisualCutouts: Record<string, string>; // slotId -> English visual description
  // Field pendukung arketipe infografis & statistik dinamis:
  statCircles?: Array<{ topLabel: string; value: string; description: string }>;
  numberedItems?: Array<{ number: string; title: string; subtitle?: string; summary: string }>;
}

export interface PosterSlotManifest {
  canvas: {
    width: number;
    height: number;
    aspectRatio: PosterAspectRatio;
  };
  editorialBlocks?: EditorialTextBlockSpec[];
  visualCutoutSlots?: VisualCutoutSlotSpec[];
  slots: {
    // Zona Subjek Tokoh Dewan (Layer 2)
    subjectPhoto: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      popOutEffect?: boolean;       // Apakah kepala/bahu menembus bingkai luar
      popOutOverflowTopPx?: number; // Jarak tembus ke atas dalam pixel
      shadowOpacity?: number;       // 0.0 - 1.0
      shadowBlur?: number;          // Blur radius dalam pixel
      duotoneTint?: string;         // Hex warna jika menggunakan mode duotone
      harmonizeFilter?: 'GRAYSCALE_HIGH_CONTRAST' | 'NATURAL';
    };
    // Zona Judul Utama (Layer 3)
    headlineBlock: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      maxLines: number;
      fontFamily: 'Bebas Neue' | 'Anton' | 'Oswald';
      fontSizePx: number;
      textColor: string;
      bgColor?: string;             // Jika menggunakan banner solid block
      isUppercase?: boolean;
      interlockingWithSubject?: boolean; // Menimpa atau berada di balik pundak
    };
    // Zona Pernyataan / Kutipan Ringkas
    quoteStatement?: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      fontFamily: string;
      fontSizePx: number;
      textColor: string;
    };
    // Zona Identitas Anggota Dewan (Nama, Gelar, Jabatan)
    memberIdentity: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      nameFontSizePx: number;
      titleFontSizePx: number;
      nameColor: string;
      titleColor: string;
      badgeStyle?: 'minimal' | 'glassmorphic' | 'solid-ribbon';
    };
    // Zona Logo Partai / Lembaga (Layer 4 - Corner Locked)
    officialEmblem: PosterSlotCoordinate & {
      maxHeightPx: number;
      placement: 'top-left' | 'top-right' | 'bottom-left' | 'bottom-right';
      showInstitutionBadge?: boolean; // Label teks pendamping (e.g., "KOMISI III // DPR RI")
    };
    // Aksen Editorial (Barcode, Stempel Edisi, Tanggal)
    editorialAccents?: {
      showBarcode?: boolean;
      barcodeCoordinate?: PosterSlotCoordinate;
      editionTag?: string;
      editionCoordinate?: PosterSlotCoordinate;
    };
  };
}

export interface PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  archetype: CreativePosterArchetype;
  referenceFile: string;
  description: string;
  tags: string[];
  aspectRatio: PosterAspectRatio;
  colorPalette: {
    primaryAccent: string;   // Warna sorotan utama (e.g., Crimson Red, Safety Orange, Gold)
    backgroundBase: string;  // Warna latar (e.g., Warm Cream #F5F2EB, Pure Black #111111)
    textColor: string;       // Warna teks utama (#FFFFFF atau #111111)
    subTextColor: string;    // Warna teks sekunder
  };
  scenePromptHint: string;   // Instruksi gaya visual untuk AI (Grain, lighting, shot perspective)
  negativeSpaceZone: string; // Instruksi ruang kosong agar tidak menabrak teks
  slotManifest: PosterSlotManifest;
}

export interface GenerateCreativePosterParams {
  topic: string;
  headline?: string;
  quote?: string;
  tenantId: string;
  memberName: string;
  memberTitle?: string;
  partyName?: string;
  partyLogoUrl?: string;
  institutionLogoUrl?: string;
  subjectPhotoUrl: string; // Foto cutout PNG transparan
  aspectRatio?: PosterAspectRatio;
  forcedArchetype?: CreativePosterArchetype;
  isOfficialDutyMode?: boolean; // True jika reses dinas (Logo DPR), False jika suara partai (Logo Partai)
}

export interface CompiledPosterPrompt {
  blueprintId: string;
  archetype: CreativePosterArchetype;
  englishPrompt: string;
  aspectRatio: PosterAspectRatio;
  canvasDimensions: { width: number; height: number };
  synthesizedCopy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
}

\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/selector.ts (63 baris, 2.4 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport { POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG } from './catalog.js';
import { CreativePosterArchetype, PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint } from './types.js';

export interface SelectBlueprintOptions {
  topic: string;
  forcedArchetype?: CreativePosterArchetype;
  excludeBlueprintId?: string; // Untuk tombol "Acak Variasi Lain" agar tidak dapat yang sama persis
}

export class PosterBlueprintSelector {
  /**
   * selectBestBlueprint melakukan Constrained RNG:
   * 1. Jika forcedArchetype diberikan, filter berdasarkan arketipe tersebut.
   * 2. Jika tidak, hitung skor relevansi berdasarkan tag blueprint terhadap topik user.
   * 3. Ambil kandidat dengan skor terbaik, lalu pilih secara acak (RNG) agar bervariasi.
   */
  public static selectBestBlueprint(options: SelectBlueprintOptions): PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint {
    const { topic, forcedArchetype, excludeBlueprintId } = options;
    const lowerTopic = (topic || '').toLowerCase();

    // 1. Filter awal (exclude template sebelumnya jika sedang di-roll ulang)
    let pool = POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG.filter((b) => b.id !== excludeBlueprintId);
    if (pool.length === 0) {
      pool = POSTER_BLUEPRINT_CATALOG;
    }

    // 2. Jika ada forcedArchetype, prioritaskan arketipe tersebut
    if (forcedArchetype) {
      const archetypePool = pool.filter((b) => b.archetype === forcedArchetype);
      if (archetypePool.length > 0) {
        const randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * archetypePool.length);
        return archetypePool[randomIndex];
      }
    }

    // 3. Keyword Scoring: Hitung kecocokan kata kunci di topik dengan tag di catalog
    const scoredPool = pool.map((blueprint) => {
      let score = 0;
      for (const tag of blueprint.tags) {
        if (lowerTopic.includes(tag.toLowerCase())) {
          score += 2;
        }
      }
      return { blueprint, score };
    });

    // 4. Cari skor tertinggi
    const maxScore = Math.max(...scoredPool.map((s) => s.score));

    // Jika ada yang cocok (score > 0), ambil semua kandidat dengan skor tertinggi
    let candidatePool = scoredPool
      .filter((s) => (maxScore > 0 ? s.score === maxScore : true))
      .map((s) => s.blueprint);

    if (candidatePool.length === 0) {
      candidatePool = pool;
    }

    // 5. Constrained Random Selection (Kocok di antara kandidat terbaik)
    const pickedIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * candidatePool.length);
    return candidatePool[pickedIndex];
  }
}
\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/creative-poster/prompt-compiler.ts (281 baris, 16.3 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport {
  PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint,
  CompiledPosterPrompt,
  SynthesizedEditorialCopy,
  SubjectCompositionMode,
} from './types.js';
import { openaiClient } from '../client.js';

export interface CompilePosterPromptOptions {
  topic: string;
  groundedContextSummary?: string; // Diambil dari hasil AutonomousResearchOrchestrator
  blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint;
  compositionMode?: SubjectCompositionMode;
  tenantBrandHex?: string;
}

export class PosterPromptCompiler {
  /**
   * TAHAP 1: Menyusun Naskah Multi-Blok & Analogi Objek Visual 1:1 menggunakan LLM (Structured JSON)
   * Menegakkan Business Rule BR-01 (Isomorfisme Panjang Karakter)
   */
  public static async synthesizeEditorialCopyAndVisuals(
    topic: string,
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint,
    groundedContext?: string
  ): Promise<SynthesizedEditorialCopy> {
    const blockConstraintsText = (blueprint.slotManifest.editorialBlocks || [])
      .map(
        (b) =>
          `- Slot ID "${b.slotId}" (Ref Asli: "${b.defaultReferenceHeader}"): Header maks ${b.constraints.headerMaxChars} karakter. Body WAJIB antara ${b.constraints.bodyMinChars} s/d ${b.constraints.bodyMaxChars} karakter.`
      )
      .join('\n');

    const visualSlotsText = (blueprint.slotManifest.visualCutoutSlots || [])
      .map(
        (v) =>
          `- Visual Slot ID "${v.slotId}": Objek referensi asli adalah "${v.originalReferenceSubject}". Terjemahkan ke objek dunia nyata di Indonesia yang relevan dengan topik "${topic}" dalam Bahasa Inggris.`
      )
      .join('\n');

    const systemPrompt = `Anda adalah Art Director & Copywriter Infografis Kebijakan POLARIS.
Tugas Anda adalah mengadaptasi tata letak poster referensi menjadi poster baru bertopik: "${topic}".

${groundedContext ? `FAKTA RISET & DATA PENDUKUNG:\n${groundedContext.slice(0, 1800)}\n` : ''}

ATURAN BISNIS MUTLAK (CHARACTER BUDGET INVARIANT):
1. Setiap blok teks harus padat, bernas, menggunakan Bahasa Indonesia baku jurnalistik.
2. PATUHI batas karakter untuk setiap Slot ID berikut agar tidak merusak grid desain:
${blockConstraintsText || '- Buat konten ringkas dan padat.'}

3. PEMETAAN OBJEK VISUAL 1:1 (DALAM BAHASA INGGRIS):
Ubah objek di poster lama menjadi objek yang relevan dengan "${topic}", namun pertahankan komposisi dan aktivitas manusianya:
${visualSlotsText}

Keluarkan output HANYA dalam format JSON murni:
{
  "mainBannerTitle": "JUDUL UTAMA 2-3 KATA TEGAS (Maks 22 karakter, contoh: CARA MENGATASI SAMPAH)",
  "mainBannerSubheader": "Subjudul pengantar di atas judul utama (Maks 65 karakter)",
  "mainBannerDefinitionBody": "Paragraf definisi/kesimpulan komprehensif di bagian paling bawah banner hitam (Wajib antara 280 s/d 360 karakter)",
  "blocks": {
    "left_positive": { "header": "...", "body": "..." },
    "left_negative_box": { "header": "...", "body": "..." },
    "center_giant_title": { "header": "2 KATA PENDEK (Maks 14 char, misal: SOLUSI NYATA)", "body": "..." },
    "right_cause": { "header": "2 KATA (Maks 20 char, misal: AKSI BERSAMA)", "body": "..." }
  },
  "statCircles": [
    { "topLabel": "Setidaknya", "value": "120+", "description": "titik lokasi terdampak langsung dalam 24 jam terakhir." },
    { "topLabel": "Armada Siaga", "value": "28 Unit", "description": "personel dan logistik darurat diterjunkan ke titik kritis." },
    { "topLabel": "Lebih dari", "value": "1.450", "description": "warga menerima bantuan terpadu di posko pelayanan." }
  ],
  "numberedItems": [
    { "number": "01", "title": "Audit Kelayakan", "subtitle": "Inspeksi Berkala", "summary": "Pemeriksaan teknis menyeluruh terhadap seluruh instalasi publik." },
    { "number": "02", "title": "Respons Cepat", "subtitle": "Satuan Reaksi 24 Jam", "summary": "Pengerahan tim terpadu untuk penanganan darurat tanpa hambatan birokrasi." },
    { "number": "03", "title": "Bantuan Terpadu", "subtitle": "Logistik & Medis", "summary": "Distribusi pangan, obat-obatan, dan posko kemanusiaan ramah keluarga." },
    { "number": "04", "title": "Pemulihan Sosial", "subtitle": "Pendampingan Warga", "summary": "Fasilitasi pemulihan dokumen kependudukan dan aset vital warga terdampak." },
    { "number": "05", "title": "Regulasi Tegas", "subtitle": "Payung Hukum Kuat", "summary": "Penerbitan perda pengawasan dan standar proteksi keselamatan lingkungan." }
  ],
  "mappedVisualCutouts": {
    "upper_right_cutout": "Indonesian sanitation workers sorting waste bins in front of a garbage collection truck",
    "mid_left_hero_cutout": "An Indonesian male sanitation worker wearing a cap and work boots, holding two large black plastic trash bags facing forward",
    "bottom_street_scene": "Indonesian urban neighborhood street scene with residents cleaning and a three-wheeled garbage collection motorcart"
  }
}`;

    try {
      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
        temperature: 0.25,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
        messages: [{ role: 'system', content: systemPrompt }],
      });

      const parsed = JSON.parse(response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}') as SynthesizedEditorialCopy;
      return this.enforceCharacterBudgets(parsed, blueprint);
    } catch (error) {
      // Fallback deterministik jika LLM timeout
      return {
        mainBannerTitle: topic.toUpperCase().slice(0, 22),
        mainBannerSubheader: `PENGEMBANGAN KEBIJAKAN PUBLIK • Fokuskajian: ${topic.slice(0, 30)}`,
        mainBannerDefinitionBody: `Secara substantif, penanganan isu ${topic} membutuhkan kolaborasi terpadu antara regulasi pemerintah daerah, pengawasan anggaran parlemen, serta partisipasi aktif masyarakat secara berkelanjutan di tingkat akar rumput.`,
        blocks: {
          left_positive: {
            header: 'DAMPAK BURUK',
            body: `Kurangnya penanganan sistematis terhadap ${topic} memicu penurunan kualitas hidup warga, menghambat produktivitas ekonomi lokal, serta memperburuk kerentanan lingkungan di wilayah terdampak.`,
          },
          left_negative_box: {
            header: 'MANFAAT PENGELOLAAN',
            body: `Tata kelola yang terstruktur mampu membuka peluang ekonomi baru, menjaga kelestarian fungsi ruang publik, meningkatkan kesehatan masyarakat, serta memperkuat ketahanan sosial daerah.`,
          },
          center_giant_title: {
            header: 'SOLUSI NYATA',
            body: `Langkah penanganan wajib dimulai dari pembenahan hulu hingga hilir melalui penyediaan infrastruktur memadai, edukasi warga secara masif, serta penegakan perda yang konsisten di lapangan.`,
          },
          right_cause: {
            header: 'AKSI BERSAMA',
            body: `Sinergi antara masyarakat, komunitas lokal, dan pemerintah daerah menjadi kunci utama dalam mengakselerasi perubahan perilaku serta memastikan keberlanjutan program di setiap kelurahan.`,
          },
        },
        statCircles: [
          { topLabel: 'Setidaknya', value: '120+', description: `lokasi atau titik krusial terdampak isu ${topic.slice(0, 20)}.` },
          { topLabel: 'Armada Siaga', value: '28 Unit', description: 'satuan tugas dan personel lapangan dikerahkan penuh.' },
          { topLabel: 'Lebih dari', value: '1.450', description: 'warga memperoleh manfaat advokasi langsung di posko.' },
        ],
        numberedItems: [
          { number: '01', title: 'Audit Kebijakan', subtitle: 'Evaluasi Komprehensif', summary: `Analisis menyeluruh terhadap tata kelola dan dampak ${topic.slice(0, 24)}.` },
          { number: '02', title: 'Aksi Lapangan', subtitle: 'Respons Langsung', summary: 'Turun ke lapangan meninjau titik kritis bersama masyarakat dan relawan.' },
          { number: '03', title: 'Penyaluran Bantuan', subtitle: 'Logistik & Layanan', summary: 'Fasilitasi bantuan nyata dan advokasi birokrasi tepat sasaran bagi warga.' },
          { number: '04', title: 'Konsolidasi Warga', subtitle: 'Partisipasi Publik', summary: 'Membangun rukun warga tanggap dan forum aspirasi akar rumput yang berdaya.' },
          { number: '05', title: 'Regulasi Perlindungan', subtitle: 'Kepastian Hukum', summary: 'Mendorong pengesahan perda perlindungan hak publik dan anggaran berpihak.' },
        ],
        mappedVisualCutouts: {
          upper_right_cutout: `Indonesian field workers and equipment addressing ${topic}`,
          mid_left_hero_cutout: `Indonesian frontline worker or citizen actively involved in ${topic}`,
          bottom_street_scene: `Authentic Indonesian street environment showing community activity related to ${topic}`,
        },
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Guardrail deterministik untuk memotong kalimat secara rapi jika LLM melebihi batas karakter
   */
  private static enforceCharacterBudgets(
    copy: SynthesizedEditorialCopy,
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint
  ): SynthesizedEditorialCopy {
    for (const spec of blueprint.slotManifest.editorialBlocks || []) {
      const target = copy.blocks?.[spec.slotId];
      if (!target) continue;

      if (target.header && target.header.length > spec.constraints.headerMaxChars) {
        target.header = target.header.slice(0, spec.constraints.headerMaxChars).trim();
      }
      if (target.body && target.body.length > spec.constraints.bodyMaxChars) {
        const truncated = target.body.slice(0, spec.constraints.bodyMaxChars - 3);
        const lastSpace = truncated.lastIndexOf(' ');
        target.body = `${truncated.slice(0, lastSpace > 0 ? lastSpace : truncated.length)}...`;
      }
    }
    return copy;
  }

  /**
   * TAHAP 2: Mengompilasi Prompt Kolase Visual (100% Tanpa Teks, Siap Di-overlay Satori)
   * Polymorphic Art Direction per Arketipe Desain
   */
  public static async compileScenePrompt(
    options: CompilePosterPromptOptions
  ): Promise<CompiledPosterPrompt> {
    const {
      topic,
      groundedContextSummary,
      blueprint,
      compositionMode = 'PUBLIC_ADVOCACY',
      tenantBrandHex,
    } = options;

    const synthesizedCopy = await this.synthesizeEditorialCopyAndVisuals(
      topic,
      blueprint,
      groundedContextSummary
    );

    const accentHex = tenantBrandHex || blueprint.colorPalette.primaryAccent || '#9E0B0F';
    const baseHex = blueprint.colorPalette.backgroundBase || '#F4F1EA';
    const cutouts = synthesizedCopy.mappedVisualCutouts || {};

    let englishPrompt = '';

    if (blueprint.archetype === 'TORN_PAPER_NEWS') {
      // Arketipe: TECHNO VERITAS / NIGHT OF TREMORS (Malam Jago Merah)
      englishPrompt = `
[ROLE & EDITORIAL PHOTOJOURNALISM ART DIRECTION]
A dramatic, high-impact photojournalistic documentary photograph in the style of Time Magazine / Reuters covering: "${topic}".
In the center foreground, emotional Indonesian community residents and emergency responders or field workers are supporting each other with deep human empathy amidst an authentic environmental or urban setting.
Behind them in the upper background, dark moody smoke-filled or atmospheric sky with glowing crimson red (${accentHex}) and amber concentric heatwave ripple rings radiating outwards.
Gritty 35mm film documentary texture, authentic cinematic lighting with rich warm embers/accents and cool twilight contrast.

[CRITICAL ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-LOGO MANDATE]
- Absolutely ZERO text, ZERO letters, ZERO words, ZERO headlines, ZERO numbers, ZERO newspaper clippings, ZERO logos, and ZERO watermarks anywhere on the image.
- Pure dramatic documentary photography only.
      `.trim();
    } else if (blueprint.archetype === 'RETRO_SPLIT_MITIGATION') {
      // Arketipe: EARTHQUAKE INFOGRAFIS RETRO STYLE (Get To Know Mitigation)
      englishPrompt = `
[ROLE & RETRO RISOGRAPH ART DIRECTION]
Vintage 1970s-1980s public safety educational risograph illustration addressing: "${topic}".
In the center, an authentic detailed illustration of Indonesian architecture, civic infrastructure, and disaster/incident response mechanisms.
Aged warm paper background (${baseHex}) with subtle halftone textures, tactile lithograph grain, muted retro color grading featuring teal (${accentHex}) and warm ochre tones.
Clean spatial layout leaving ample breathing room for diagrammatic callouts.

[CRITICAL ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-LOGO MANDATE]
- DO NOT generate any text, letters, titles, signs, diagrams, numbers, or logos.
- Pure illustrative artwork only.
      `.trim();
    } else if (blueprint.archetype === 'MODULAR_GRID_CARDS') {
      // Arketipe: 5 MODERN FONT PAIRINGS (Modular 5 Cards)
      englishPrompt = `
[ROLE & MODERN MONOCHROME MINIMALIST ART DIRECTION]
High-end editorial architectural and documentary contact sheet in high-contrast black and white for: "${topic}".
A panoramic or quadrant composition capturing modern Indonesian civic infrastructure, public spaces, environmental protection facilities, and community collaboration.
Crisp Hasselblad monochrome photography, razor-sharp focus, rich deep blacks and clean highlights, subtle cinematic film grain.

[CRITICAL ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-LOGO MANDATE]
- DO NOT generate any numbers, text, letters, labels, or watermarks.
- Pure photographic visual texture only.
      `.trim();
    } else if (blueprint.archetype === 'VINTAGE_PERSONA_GAZETTE') {
      // Arketipe: THE S NEWS / BIOGRAFI TOKOH
      englishPrompt = `
[ROLE & ARCHIVAL BROADSHEET ART DIRECTION]
Warm archival broadsheet newspaper background texture and environmental portrait setting for: "${topic}".
Vintage sepia-toned darkroom lighting with subtle paper grain, aged print patina, and soft vignette.
Atmospheric and dignified civic leadership mood.

[CRITICAL ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-LOGO MANDATE]
- DO NOT generate any headlines, newsprint letters, mastheads, or logos.
      `.trim();
    } else {
      // Arketipe: STREET_JOURNALISM (Poster PKL / Risograph Default)
      const midLeftDirective =
        compositionMode === 'FIGURE_CENTERPIECE'
          ? `- MIDDLE-LEFT ZONE (x: 15%-45%, y: 28%-70%): Paint ONLY a dramatic diagonal crimson red (${accentHex}) brush-stroke splash and subtle black wireframe wave lines on the cream paper. Leave the center of this brush splash EMPTY without any person so an external portrait cutout can be placed on top.`
          : `- MIDDLE-LEFT FOREGROUND (x: 15%-45%, y: 28%-72%): A striking high-contrast black-and-white halftone cutout photograph of ${cutouts.mid_left_hero_cutout || 'an Indonesian community worker'}, standing prominently in front of a bold diagonal crimson red (${accentHex}) brush-stroke splash.`;

      englishPrompt = `
[ROLE & BRUTALIST EDITORIAL ART DIRECTION]
Act as a master Indonesian graphic designer creating a vintage brutalist newspaper collage poster background (vertical 4:5 ratio, 1080x1350px) for the topic: "${topic}".

[EXACT SPATIAL COLLAGE BLUEPRINT - FOLLOW COORDINATES STRICTLY]
1. PAPER BASE (UPPER 74% OF CANVAS):
   - Textured vintage off-white newsprint paper (${baseHex}) with subtle archival grain.
   - Completely clean, empty paper zones in the Top-Left (x: 5%-38%, y: 5%-36%), Top-Center (x: 40%-58%, y: 5%-46%), and Middle-Right (x: 60%-94%, y: 28%-48%) to serve as clean negative space for external typography overlays.

2. CUTOUT PHOTOGRAPHY & GRAPHIC ACCENTS (BLACK & WHITE HALFTONE + CRIMSON RED):
   - UPPER-RIGHT QUADRANT (x: 60%-96%, y: 4%-27%): High-contrast black-and-white documentary cutout photo of ${cutouts.upper_right_cutout || 'Indonesian public service activity'}, overlapping a geometric crimson red (${accentHex}) shape on the right edge.
   ${midLeftDirective}
   - LOWER-MIDDLE STREET MONTAGE (x: 0%-100%, y: 48%-74%): Gritty, high-contrast black-and-white Indonesian architectural street scene featuring ${cutouts.bottom_street_scene || 'urban civic life'}. Include a flowing crimson red (${accentHex}) ribbon wave and fine black topographic contour lines across the bottom-right street corner.

3. SOLID BLACK FOOTER BLOCK (BOTTOM 26% OF CANVAS):
   - The bottom section (y: 74%-100%) MUST be a flat, solid pitch-black (#0A0A0A) rectangular banner across the entire width.

[CRITICAL ZERO-TEXT / ZERO-LOGO MANDATE]
- DO NOT generate any text, letters, numbers, newspaper headlines, signboards, license plates, or typography anywhere on the image.
- DO NOT generate any logos, emblems, or watermarks.
- Output ONLY the clean visual collage art and empty negative-space zones ready for programmatic SVG text compositing.
`.trim();
    }

    const { width, height } = blueprint.slotManifest.canvas;

    return {
      blueprintId: blueprint.id,
      archetype: blueprint.archetype,
      englishPrompt,
      aspectRatio: blueprint.aspectRatio,
      canvasDimensions: { width, height },
      synthesizedCopy,
    };
  }
}

\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/ai-engine.adapter.ts (402 baris, 12.6 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport {
  ILLMProviderPort,
  GenerateArticleParams,
  GeneratedArticleResult,
  GeneratedSocialPackResult,
  GenerateCivicAnswerParams,
  GeneratePublicConciergeParams,
  GeneratedConciergeResult,
} from '@polaris/core-domain';
import { InfographicDataSpecDto } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { openaiClient } from './client.js';
import { PromptTemplates } from './prompts.js';
import { PromptSanitizer } from './guardrails/prompt-sanitizer.js';
import { AiTelemetryService } from './telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';
import { TwoStageArticleEngine } from './two-stage-article.engine.js';
import {
  PosterBlueprintSelector,
  PosterPromptCompiler,
  CreativePosterArchetype,
  PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint,
  CompiledPosterPrompt,
} from './creative-poster/index.js';

export class OpenAIAIEngineAdapter implements ILLMProviderPort {

  public async generatePublicConcierge(
    params: GeneratePublicConciergeParams
  ): Promise<GeneratedConciergeResult> {
    const inspection = PromptSanitizer.inspect(params.userMessage);

    // Jika tertolak oleh pre-inference guardrail, catat sebagai REFUSAL (0 token, 0 cost)
    if (!inspection.isValid) {
      const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
        operation: 'public-concierge-chat',
        model: 'heuristic-guardrail',
        metadata: { rejectionReason: inspection.rejectionReason },
      });

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'REFUSAL',
        errorMessage: inspection.rejectionReason,
        outputPreview: inspection.preBakedReply,
      });

      return {
        reply: inspection.preBakedReply || 'Pertanyaan tidak dapat diproses.',
        suggestedAction: inspection.suggestedAction,
        isSafeRefusal: true,
        tokensUsed: 0,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    }

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'public-concierge-chat',
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      metadata: { rawInputLength: params.userMessage.length },
    });

    try {
      const historyContext = (params.history || [])
        .slice(-3)
        .map((h) => `${h.role === 'user' ? 'Pengunjung' : 'Asisten'}: ${h.content.slice(0, 150)}`)
        .join('\n');

      const systemPrompt = PromptTemplates.getPublicConciergePrompt(
        inspection.sanitizedText,
        historyContext
      );

      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
        temperature: 0.25,
        max_tokens: 300,
        messages: [
          { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
          { role: 'user', content: inspection.sanitizedText },
        ],
      });

      const reply = response.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || '';
      const inputTokens = response.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0;
      const outputTokens = response.usage?.completion_tokens || 0;

      const isRefusal = reply.toLowerCase().includes('hanya dapat membantu menjawab pertanyaan seputar') ||
                        reply.toLowerCase().includes('diprogram khusus untuk memberikan informasi seputar');

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens,
        outputTokens,
        status: isRefusal ? 'REFUSAL' : 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: reply,
      });

      return {
        reply,
        suggestedAction: inspection.suggestedAction,
        isSafeRefusal: isRefusal,
        tokensUsed: inputTokens + outputTokens,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message,
      });

      return {
        reply: 'Mohon maaf, sistem asisten POLARIS sedang mengalami antrean jaringan. Silakan segarkan halaman.',
        suggestedAction: 'NONE',
        isSafeRefusal: true,
        tokensUsed: 0,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    }
  }

  public async generateArticle(params: GenerateArticleParams): Promise<GeneratedArticleResult> {
    return TwoStageArticleEngine.execute(params);
  }

  public async generateDalleImage(
    englishPrompt: string,
    tenantId?: string,
    size: '1024x1792' | '1024x1024' | '1792x1024' = '1024x1792'
  ): Promise<{ temporaryImageUrl: string; canonicalTraceId: string }> {
    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'dalle-image-generation',
      model: 'dall-e-3',
      tenantId,
      metadata: { promptLength: englishPrompt.length, size },
    });

    const preferredModel = process.env.OPENAI_IMAGE_MODEL || 'dall-e-3';
    let activeModel = preferredModel;
    try {
      let temporaryImageUrl: string | undefined;

      const response = await openaiClient.images.generate({
        model: preferredModel as any,
        prompt: englishPrompt,
        size,
        quality: 'standard',
        n: 1,
      });
      const first = response.data?.[0];
      if (first?.url) {
        temporaryImageUrl = first.url;
      } else if (first?.b64_json) {
        temporaryImageUrl = `data:image/png;base64,${first.b64_json}`;
      }

      if (!temporaryImageUrl) {
        throw new Error('AI Generator tidak mengembalikan URL atau data gambar yang valid.');
      }

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 1000,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: { temporaryImageUrl: temporaryImageUrl.slice(0, 100), model: activeModel },
      });

      return {
        temporaryImageUrl,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message,
      });

      throw new Error(`[AIEngineDalleError] Gagal me-render poster DALL-E: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * generateCreativePosterBackground memilih template secara Constrained RNG,
   * mengompilasi prompt fotografi clean-art tanpa teks, dan menghasilkan background AI.
   */
  public async generateCreativePosterBackground(params: {
    topic: string;
    groundedContextSummary?: string;
    compositionMode?: import('./creative-poster/types.js').SubjectCompositionMode;
    forcedArchetype?: CreativePosterArchetype;
    excludeBlueprintId?: string;
    tenantBrandHex?: string;
    tenantId?: string;
  }): Promise<{
    temporaryImageUrl: string;
    blueprint: PreAnalyzedPosterBlueprint;
    compiledPrompt: CompiledPosterPrompt;
    canonicalTraceId: string;
  }> {
    const blueprint = PosterBlueprintSelector.selectBestBlueprint({
      topic: params.topic,
      forcedArchetype: params.forcedArchetype,
      excludeBlueprintId: params.excludeBlueprintId,
    });

    const compiledPrompt = await PosterPromptCompiler.compileScenePrompt({
      topic: params.topic,
      groundedContextSummary: params.groundedContextSummary,
      compositionMode: params.compositionMode,
      blueprint,
      tenantBrandHex: params.tenantBrandHex,
    });

    const targetSize =
      compiledPrompt.aspectRatio === '9:16' || compiledPrompt.aspectRatio === '4:5'
        ? '1024x1792'
        : '1024x1024';

    const imageResult = await this.generateDalleImage(
      compiledPrompt.englishPrompt,
      params.tenantId,
      targetSize
    );

    return {
      temporaryImageUrl: imageResult.temporaryImageUrl,
      blueprint,
      compiledPrompt,
      canonicalTraceId: imageResult.canonicalTraceId,
    };
  }

  public async generateCivicAnswer(
    params: GenerateCivicAnswerParams
  ): Promise<{ answer: string; canonicalTraceId: string }> {
    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'generate-civic-answer-grounded',
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      tenantId: params.tenantId,
      metadata: { representative: params.representativeName, dapil: params.dapilName },
    });

    try {
      const systemPrompt = PromptTemplates.getCivicAssistantPrompt(
        params.groundedRegulations,
        params.representativeName,
        params.partyAffiliation,
        params.dapilName
      );

      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
        temperature: 0.2,
        max_tokens: 300,
        messages: [
          { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
          { role: 'user', content: params.citizenQuestion },
        ],
      });

      const answer = response.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || '';
      const inputTokens = response.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0;
      const outputTokens = response.usage?.completion_tokens || 0;

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens,
        outputTokens,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: answer,
      });

      return {
        answer,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message,
      });

      throw new Error(`[AIEngineCivicAnswerError] Gagal menyusun respon warga: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  public async generateEmbedding(
    text: string,
    tenantId?: string
  ): Promise<{ embedding: number[]; canonicalTraceId: string }> {
    const cleanText = text.trim();
    if (!cleanText) {
      throw new Error('[EmbeddingError] Teks input untuk embedding tidak boleh kosong.');
    }

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'text-embedding-rag',
      model: 'text-embedding-3-small',
      tenantId,
      metadata: { textLength: cleanText.length },
    });

    try {
      const response = await openaiClient.embeddings.create({
        model: 'text-embedding-3-small',
        input: cleanText,
      });

      const embedding = response.data[0]?.embedding;
      if (!embedding || embedding.length !== 1536) {
        throw new Error('Dimensi embedding yang dikembalikan tidak sesuai.');
      }

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: response.usage.total_tokens,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: { dimensions: embedding.length },
      });

      return {
        embedding,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message,
      });

      throw new Error(`[AIEngineEmbeddingError] Gagal menghasilkan embedding: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  public async generateInfographicSpec(articleContent: string, tenantId?: string): Promise<InfographicDataSpecDto> {
    const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      temperature: 0.2,
      response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
      messages: [
        { role: 'system', content: PromptTemplates.getInfographicSystemPrompt() },
        { role: 'user', content: `Artikel rujukan:\n\n${articleContent}` },
      ],
    });

    return JSON.parse(response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}');
  }

  public async generateDallePrompt(infographicSpec: InfographicDataSpecDto): Promise<string> {
    return PromptTemplates.getDalleVisualPrompt(infographicSpec.headline, infographicSpec.policyTakeaway);
  }

  public async generateSocialSnippets(articleContent: string): Promise<GeneratedSocialPackResult> {
    const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      temperature: 0.4,
      response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
      messages: [
        { role: 'system', content: PromptTemplates.getSocialSnippetSystemPrompt() },
        { role: 'user', content: `Artikel rujukan:\n\n${articleContent}` },
      ],
    });

    return JSON.parse(response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}');
  }

  public async performVisionOCR(params: {
    base64Data: string;
    mimeType: string;
    fileName: string;
  }): Promise<string> {
    const imageUrl = params.base64Data.startsWith('data:')
      ? params.base64Data
      : `data:${params.mimeType};base64,${params.base64Data}`;

    const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      temperature: 0.1,
      max_tokens: 2000,
      messages: [
        {
          role: 'system',
          content: 'Ekstrak seluruh teks dan data tabel dari dokumen lampiran ke dalam format Markdown rapi.',
        },
        {
          role: 'user',
          content: [
            { type: 'text', text: `Transkripsikan isi dokumen "${params.fileName}":` },
            { type: 'image_url', image_url: { url: imageUrl, detail: 'high' } },
          ],
        },
      ],
    });

    return response.choices[0]?.message?.content || 'Tidak ada teks yang terdeteksi.';
  }
}
\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/prompts.ts (128 baris, 7.8 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport { ConciergePromptBuilder } from './prompts/concierge.prompt.js';

export class PromptTemplates {
  public static getPublicConciergePrompt(userMessage: string, historyContext = ''): string {
    return ConciergePromptBuilder.buildPrompt(userMessage, historyContext);
  }

  public static getCivicAssistantPrompt(
    groundedRegulations: string,
    representativeName: string,
    partyAffiliation?: string | null,
    dapilName?: string | null
  ): string {
    return `Anda adalah "POLARIS Civic Assistant", representasi digital resmi dari ${representativeName} (Fraksi ${partyAffiliation || 'Parlemen'}, Dapil ${dapilName || 'Daerah Pemilihan'}).

TUGAS UTAMA:
Menjawab pertanyaan warga dengan ramah, lugas, solutif, dan berwibawa, berdasarkan rujukan hukum dan regulasi resmi yang tersedia.

KONTEKS REGULASI DAERAH YANG DITEMUKAN (SUMBER KEBENARAN TUNGGAL):
${groundedRegulations || 'Tidak ditemukan pasal regulasi daerah spesifik yang relevan dengan pertanyaan ini.'}

PANDUAN MENJAWAB:
1. Batasi jawaban maksimal 3 hingga 4 kalimat yang padat dan informatif.
2. JIKA regulasi yang ditemukan relevan, sebutkan nomor pasal/perda terkait secara ringkas sebagai dasar jawaban.
3. JIKA konteks regulasi kosong atau tidak menjawab pertanyaan secara presisi, JANGAN MENGARANG ATURAN FIKTIF. 4. Akhiri dengan salam hormat khas pelayanan publik yang ramah.`;

9. Format Output wajib berupa JSON terstruktur murni tanpa markdown pembungkus.`;
  }
