\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/guardrails/prompt-sanitizer.ts (66 baris, 2.8 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nexport interface SanitizeResult {
    isValid: boolean;
    sanitizedText: string;
    rejectionReason?: string;
    preBakedReply?: string;
    suggestedAction: 'VIEW_PRICING' | 'REGISTER' | 'NONE';
}

export class PromptSanitizer {
    public static readonly MAX_INPUT_CHARS = 250;

    private static readonly INJECTION_PATTERNS: RegExp[] = [
        /ignore\s+(all\s+)?(previous\s+)?instructions/i,
        /abaikan\s+(semua\s+)?(instruksi|perintah|aturan)/i,
        /dan\s+mode/i,
        /jailbreak/i,
        /pretend\s+you\s+are/i,
        /bertindaklah\s+sebagai/i,
        /you\s+are\s+now\s+an?\s+unrestricted/i,
        /(system\s+prompt|instruksi\s+sistem)/i,
        /(prompt|system)\s+dump/i,
        /(tuliskan|cetak|print|show|bocorkan)\s+(system\s+prompt|instruksi\s+sistem)/i,
        /bikin(kan)?\s+(kode|script|program|python|javascript|sql)/i,
        /write\s+(a\s+)?(script|code|malware|exploit)/i,
    ];

    private static readonly OFF_TOPIC_PATTERNS: RegExp[] = [
        /\b(siapa|who\s+is)\s+(presiden|president|perdana\s+menteri|prime\s+minister)\b/i,
        /\b(resep|masak|recipe|cook(ing)?|cuaca|weather|matematika|mathematics|\bmath\b)\b/i,
        /\b(berapa\s+hasil|calculate|solve\s+this)\b/i,
    ];

    public static inspect(rawText: string): SanitizeResult {
        if (!rawText || rawText.trim().length === 0) {
            return {
                isValid: false,
                sanitizedText: '',
                rejectionReason: 'Pesan kosong.',
                preBakedReply: 'Halo! Ada yang bisa saya bantu terkait fitur, layanan, atau paket lisensi POLARIS?',
                suggestedAction: 'NONE',
            };
        }

        const clamped = rawText.trim().slice(0, this.MAX_INPUT_CHARS);
        if ([...this.INJECTION_PATTERNS, ...this.OFF_TOPIC_PATTERNS].some((pattern) => pattern.test(clamped))) {
            return {
                isValid: false,
                sanitizedText: clamped,
                rejectionReason: 'Pola manipulasi prompt atau pertanyaan di luar konteks terdeteksi.',
                preBakedReply:
                    'Mohon maaf, saya adalah asisten resmi POLARIS yang diprogram khusus untuk memberikan informasi seputar fitur, paket harga, dan layanan platform POLARIS.',
                suggestedAction: 'NONE',
            };
        }

        const lower = clamped.toLowerCase();
        let suggestedAction: SanitizeResult['suggestedAction'] = 'NONE';
        if (['harga', 'biaya', 'paket', 'tarif', 'bayar'].some((word) => lower.includes(word))) {
            suggestedAction = 'VIEW_PRICING';
        } else if (['daftar', 'register', 'buat akun', 'coba'].some((word) => lower.includes(word))) {
            suggestedAction = 'REGISTER';
        }

        return { isValid: true, sanitizedText: clamped, suggestedAction };
    }
}\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/context-capsule.ts (58 baris, 2.7 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nexport interface RawContextSources {
  regionName: string;
  regulations: Array<{ reference: string; content: string }>;
  recentNews: Array<{ title: string; summary: string; portal: string }>;
  memberWritingSample?: string;
  comparisonRegionData?: string;
  externalWebSources?: Array<{ url: string; title?: string; content: string }>;
  attachedDocuments?: Array<{ name: string; type: string; extractedContent: string }>;
  framingStance?: string;
}

export class ContextCapsuleBuilder {
  /**
   * assembleContext menggabungkan seluruh layer fakta menjadi satu teks
   * grounding terstruktur untuk disuntikkan ke LLM.
   */
  public static assembleContext(sources: RawContextSources): string {
    const regulationSection = sources.regulations.length > 0
      ? sources.regulations.map((r, i) => `[Regulasi ${i + 1}] ${r.reference}:\n${r.content}`).join('\n\n')
      : 'Data regulasi daerah khusus belum tersedia.';

    const newsSection = sources.recentNews.length > 0
      ? sources.recentNews.map((n, i) => `[Berita ${i + 1} - ${n.portal}] ${n.title}: ${n.summary}`).join('\n')
      : 'Tidak ada berita daerah signifikan dalam 24 jam terakhir.';

    const comparisonSection = sources.comparisonRegionData
      ? `\n\n--- DATA KOMPARASI REGIONAL (STUDI BANDING) ---\n${sources.comparisonRegionData}`
      : '';

    const writingStyleSection = sources.memberWritingSample
      ? `\n\n--- CONTOH PREFERENSI GAYA BAHASA RESMI DEWAN ---\n"${sources.memberWritingSample}"\n(Catatan: Pertahankan diksi dan bobot argumentasi serupa dengan contoh ini).`
      : '';

    const webSection = sources.externalWebSources && sources.externalWebSources.length > 0
      ? `\n\n--- RUJUKAN SITUS WEB & RISET INTERNET TERKINI (LIVE INGESTION) ---\n` +
        sources.externalWebSources.map((w, i) => `[Tautan Web ${i + 1}] ${w.url}${w.title ? ` (${w.title})` : ''}:\n${w.content}`).join('\n\n')
      : '';

    const docSection = sources.attachedDocuments && sources.attachedDocuments.length > 0
      ? `\n\n--- DOKUMEN & FOTO LAMPIRAN DEWAN (HASIL OCR & EKSTRAKSI DATA TABEL) ---\n` +
        sources.attachedDocuments.map((d, i) => `[Dokumen ${i + 1}] ${d.name} (${d.type}):\n${d.extractedContent}`).join('\n\n')
      : '';

    const framingSection = sources.framingStance
      ? `\n\n--- ARAHAN SUDUT PANDANG / FRAMING POLITIK DEWAN ---\nFokus Framing: ${sources.framingStance}`
      : '';

    return `=== KAPSUL KONTEKS POLARIS (GROUNDING DATA LENGKAP) ===
WILAYAH TARGET: ${sources.regionName}

--- FAKTA REGULASI & PERATURAN RESMI AKTIF ---
${regulationSection}

--- DISKURSUS PUBLIK & BERITA MEDIA TERKINI (CRON CRAWLER) ---
${newsSection}${webSection}${docSection}${framingSection}${comparisonSection}${writingStyleSection}
======================================================`;
  }
}
\n\n================================================================================\n# BAGIAN: 3. AUTONOMOUS RESEARCH & QUALITY GATES (6 Berkas)\n================================================================================\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/autonomous-research.orchestrator.ts (529 baris, 17.4 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport {
  db,
  researchJobs,
  researchQuestions,
  sources,
  researchJobSources,
  sourceVersions,
  sourceAssessments,
  evidenceSpans,
  claims,
  claimEvidence,
  researchOutputs,
  eq,
} from '@polaris/database';
import {
  ResearchJobStatus,
  SourceType,
  PublisherType,
  SourceLifecycle,
  ClaimStatus,
  SupportType,
} from '@polaris/shared-types';

import { ResearchPlannerEngine, ResearchPlanInput, ResearchPlanOutput } from './research-planner.engine.js';

import { AcademicDiscoveryConnector } from './connectors/academic-discovery.connector.js';
import { InternalKnowledgeConnector } from './connectors/internal-knowledge.connector.js';
import { WebDiscoveryConnector } from './connectors/web-discovery.connector.js';
import { SourceCredibilityAssessor } from './source-credibility.assessor.js';
import { ClaimEvidenceExtractor } from './claim-evidence.extractor.js';
import { DeterministicQualityGates } from './deterministic-gates.js';

export interface AutonomousResearchJobInput {
  tenantId: string;
  topic: string;
  userPrompt: string;
  targetRegion: string;
  queryEmbedding?: number[];
  attachedDocuments?: Array<{ name: string; type: string; extractedContent: string }>;
  providedUrls?: string[];
  onProgress?: (step: string, percent: number) => Promise<void> | void;
}

export interface AutonomousResearchJobResult {
  jobId: string;
  mainTopic: string;
  evidenceSufficient: boolean;
  qcPassed: boolean;
  totalSourcesEvaluated: number;
  verifiedSourcesCount: number;
  totalEvidenceSpans: number;
  claimsCount: number;
  groundedContextText: string;
  evidenceIdMap: Map<string, string>; // Maps "EV-1" to database UUID
}

export class AutonomousResearchOrchestrator {
  /**
   * Menjalankan siklus riset otonom POLARIS secara menyeluruh (End-to-End Autonomous Research)
   */
  public static async executeResearchPipeline(
    input: AutonomousResearchJobInput
  ): Promise<AutonomousResearchJobResult> {
    const emit = async (msg: string, pct: number) => {
      if (input.onProgress) {
        await input.onProgress(msg, pct);
      }
    };

    // 1. Inisialisasi Job Riset di Basis Data
    await emit('Menginisialisasi sesi riset otonom di database...', 5);
    const [job] = await db
      .insert(researchJobs)
      .values({
        tenantId: input.tenantId,
        topic: input.topic,
        userPrompt: input.userPrompt,
        targetRegion: input.targetRegion,
        status: ResearchJobStatus.PLANNING,
      })
      .returning();

    const jobId = job.id;

    // 2. TAHAP 1: Research Planner (Divide-and-Conquer Questions)
    await emit('Merumuskan pertanyaan riset kausal & parameter bukti...', 12);
    const attachedSummary = (input.attachedDocuments || [])
      .map((d) => `[${d.name}]: ${d.extractedContent.slice(0, 500)}`)
      .join('\n');

    const plan: ResearchPlanOutput = await ResearchPlannerEngine.createPlan({
      userPrompt: input.userPrompt,
      targetRegion: input.targetRegion,
      attachedDocsSummary: attachedSummary,
      providedUrls: input.providedUrls,
      tenantId: input.tenantId,
    });

    // Simpan daftar pertanyaan riset ke tabel research_questions
    const insertedQuestions: Array<{ id: string; question: string; minRequiredEvidence: number }> = [];
    for (const q of plan.questions) {
      const [qRow] = await db
        .insert(researchQuestions)
        .values({
          researchJobId: jobId,
          question: q.question,
          intentCategory: q.intentCategory,
          temporalBound: q.temporalBound,
          geographicScope: q.geographicScope,
          minRequiredEvidence: q.minRequiredEvidence || 1,
        })
        .returning();
      insertedQuestions.push({
        id: qRow.id,
        question: qRow.question,
        minRequiredEvidence: qRow.minRequiredEvidence,
      });
    }

    // 3. TAHAP 2: Multi-Source Discovery (Internal Knowledge + Web + Academic Crossref)
    await db
      .update(researchJobs)
      .set({ status: ResearchJobStatus.SEARCHING, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(researchJobs.id, jobId));

    await emit('Mengambil regulasi internal daerah & arsip diskursus berita...', 22);
    const internalKnowledge = await InternalKnowledgeConnector.retrieveInternalKnowledge({
      queryEmbedding: input.queryEmbedding,
      targetRegion: input.targetRegion,
      policySector: plan.internalRetrievalPlan.mediaSector,
      keywords: plan.internalRetrievalPlan.mediaKeywords,
      maxRegulations: 3,
      maxNews: 4,
    });

    await emit('Menelusuri publikasi ilmiah bereputasi (Crossref DOI) & tautan web...', 32);
    const academicPapers = plan.academicQueries && plan.academicQueries.length > 0
      ? await AcademicDiscoveryConnector.searchCrossref(plan.academicQueries[0], 3)
      : [];

    const webCandidates = input.providedUrls && input.providedUrls.length > 0
      ? await WebDiscoveryConnector.fetchMultipleUrls(input.providedUrls)
      : [];

    // 4. TAHAP 3: Source Registry, Deduplication & Credibility Assessment
    await db
      .update(researchJobs)
      .set({ status: ResearchJobStatus.EVALUATING, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(researchJobs.id, jobId));

    await emit('Menilai substansi, kesegaran & integritas metodologi sumber...', 42);

    interface RegisteredSourceItem {
      sourceId: string;
      versionId: string;
      title: string;
      url: string;
      publisherName: string;
      extractedText: string;
      isAccepted: boolean;
    }

    const evaluatedSources: RegisteredSourceItem[] = [];

    // Proses A: Registrasi Sumber Regulasi Internal
    for (const reg of internalKnowledge.regulations) {
      const [s] = await db
        .insert(sources)
        .values({
          url: `internal://regulation/${reg.documentId}`,
          canonicalUrl: `internal://regulation/${reg.documentId}`,
          sourceType: SourceType.INTERNAL_REGULATION,
          publisherName: 'Pemerintah Daerah / JDIH',
          publisherType: PublisherType.GOVERNMENT,
          publisherCredibilityScore: 0.95,
          lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED,
        })
        .returning();

      const [sv] = await db
        .insert(sourceVersions)
        .values({
          sourceId: s.id,
          contentHash: WebDiscoveryConnector.hashContent(reg.chunkContent),
          title: reg.structuralReference,
          extractedText: reg.chunkContent,
          httpStatus: 200,
        })
        .returning();

      await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
        researchJobId: jobId,
        sourceId: s.id,
        relevanceScore: reg.similarityScore,
        queryUsed: 'Vector HNSW Cosine Search',
      });

      evaluatedSources.push({
        sourceId: s.id,
        versionId: sv.id,
        title: reg.structuralReference,
        url: s.url,
        publisherName: 'JDIH / Regulasi Daerah',
        extractedText: reg.chunkContent,
        isAccepted: true,
      });
    }

    // Proses B: Registrasi Sumber Berita Internal (media_discourses)
    for (const news of internalKnowledge.news) {
      const [s] = await db
        .insert(sources)
        .values({
          url: news.originalUrl,
          canonicalUrl: news.originalUrl,
          sourceType: SourceType.INTERNAL_NEWS,
          publisherName: news.portalName,
          publisherType: PublisherType.MEDIA,
          publisherCredibilityScore: 0.80,
          lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED,
        })
        .returning();

      const [sv] = await db
        .insert(sourceVersions)
        .values({
          sourceId: s.id,
          contentHash: WebDiscoveryConnector.hashContent(news.cleanSummary),
          title: news.articleTitle,
          extractedText: news.cleanSummary,
          httpStatus: 200,
        })
        .returning();

      await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
        researchJobId: jobId,
        sourceId: s.id,
        relevanceScore: 0.80,
        queryUsed: 'Civic Media Diskursus Filter',
      });

      evaluatedSources.push({
        sourceId: s.id,
        versionId: sv.id,
        title: news.articleTitle,
        url: s.url,
        publisherName: news.portalName,
        extractedText: news.cleanSummary,
        isAccepted: true,
      });
    }

    // Proses C: Asesmen dan Registrasi Paper Akademik Crossref
    for (const paper of academicPapers) {
      const [s] = await db
        .insert(sources)
        .values({
          url: paper.url,
          canonicalUrl: `https://doi.org/${paper.doi}`,
          sourceType: SourceType.ACADEMIC_PAPER,
          publisherName: paper.journalTitle || 'Academic Publisher',
          publisherType: PublisherType.ACADEMIC,
          doi: paper.doi,
          isPeerReviewed: paper.isPeerReviewed,
          hasRetractionsOrCorrections: paper.hasRetractionsOrCorrections,
          publisherCredibilityScore: 0.88,
          lifecycleStatus: paper.hasRetractionsOrCorrections
            ? SourceLifecycle.REJECTED
            : SourceLifecycle.ASSESSED,
        })
        .returning();

      const textBody = `${paper.title}. Penulis: ${paper.authors.join(', ')} (${paper.publicationYear || 'N/A'}). Jurnal: ${paper.journalTitle || ''}. ${paper.abstractSnippet || ''}`;
      const [sv] = await db
        .insert(sourceVersions)
        .values({
          sourceId: s.id,
          contentHash: WebDiscoveryConnector.hashContent(textBody),
          title: paper.title,
          extractedText: textBody,
          httpStatus: 200,
        })
        .returning();

      const assessment = await SourceCredibilityAssessor.assessSource(
        {
          url: paper.url,
          domain: 'crossref.org',
          title: paper.title,
          extractedText: textBody,
          publisherType: PublisherType.ACADEMIC,
          doi: paper.doi,
          isPeerReviewed: paper.isPeerReviewed,
          hasRetractions: paper.hasRetractionsOrCorrections,
        },
        plan.mainTopic
      );

      await db.insert(sourceAssessments).values({
        sourceId: s.id,
        substantiveScore: assessment.substantiveScore,
        methodologyScore: assessment.methodologyScore,
        recencyScore: assessment.recencyScore,
        overallScore: assessment.overallScore,
        assessmentReason: assessment.assessmentReason,
        isAccepted: assessment.isAccepted,
      });

      await db
        .update(sources)
        .set({ lifecycleStatus: assessment.lifecycleStatus })
        .where(eq(sources.id, s.id));

      await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
        researchJobId: jobId,
        sourceId: s.id,
        relevanceScore: assessment.overallScore,
        queryUsed: plan.academicQueries[0] || 'Academic Query',
      });

      evaluatedSources.push({
        sourceId: s.id,
        versionId: sv.id,
        title: paper.title,
        url: paper.url,
        publisherName: paper.journalTitle || 'Jurnal Akademik',
        extractedText: textBody,
        isAccepted: assessment.isAccepted,
      });
    }

    // Proses D: Asesmen dan Registrasi Tautan Web Eksternal
    for (const web of webCandidates) {
      const [s] = await db
        .insert(sources)
        .values({
          url: web.url,
          canonicalUrl: web.canonicalUrl,
          sourceType: SourceType.EXTERNAL_WEB,
          publisherName: web.publisherName,
          publisherType: PublisherType.MEDIA,
          lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.FETCHED,
        })
        .returning();

      const [sv] = await db
        .insert(sourceVersions)
        .values({
          sourceId: s.id,
          contentHash: web.contentHash,
          title: web.title,
          rawContent: web.rawContent,
          extractedText: web.extractedText,
          httpStatus: web.httpStatus,
        })
        .returning();

      const assessment = await SourceCredibilityAssessor.assessSource(
        {
          url: web.url,
          domain: web.domain,
          title: web.title,
          extractedText: web.extractedText,
          publisherType: PublisherType.MEDIA,
        },
        plan.mainTopic
      );

      await db.insert(sourceAssessments).values({
        sourceId: s.id,
        substantiveScore: assessment.substantiveScore,
        methodologyScore: assessment.methodologyScore,
        recencyScore: assessment.recencyScore,
        overallScore: assessment.overallScore,
        assessmentReason: assessment.assessmentReason,
        isAccepted: assessment.isAccepted,
      });

      await db
        .update(sources)
        .set({ lifecycleStatus: assessment.lifecycleStatus })
        .where(eq(sources.id, s.id));

      await db.insert(researchJobSources).values({
        researchJobId: jobId,
        sourceId: s.id,
        relevanceScore: assessment.overallScore,
        queryUsed: 'User Supplied URL',
      });

      evaluatedSources.push({
        sourceId: s.id,
        versionId: sv.id,
        title: web.title,
        url: web.url,
        publisherName: web.publisherName || web.domain,
        extractedText: web.extractedText,
        isAccepted: assessment.isAccepted,
      });
    }

    // 5. TAHAP 4: Claim & Evidence Extraction
    await db
      .update(researchJobs)
      .set({ status: ResearchJobStatus.EXTRACTING, updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(eq(researchJobs.id, jobId));

    await emit('Mengekstrak klaim kebijakan & bukti kutipan verbatim...', 58);

    const acceptedSources = evaluatedSources.filter((s) => s.isAccepted);
    const extractedClaims = await ClaimEvidenceExtractor.extractClaimsAndEvidence(
      plan.mainTopic,
      insertedQuestions.map((q, idx) => ({ index: idx + 1, question: q.question })),
      acceptedSources
    );

    // Simpan claims dan evidence_spans ke DB
    const evidenceCountPerQuestion: Record<string, number> = {};
    const evidenceIdMap = new Map<string, string>(); // 'EV-1' -> database uuid
    let spanCounter = 1;

    for (const c of extractedClaims) {
      // Petakan ke researchQuestionId
      const targetQuestion =
        c.questionIndex && c.questionIndex > 0 && c.questionIndex <= insertedQuestions.length
          ? insertedQuestions[c.questionIndex - 1]
          : insertedQuestions[0];

      const [claimRow] = await db
        .insert(claims)
        .values({
          researchJobId: jobId,
          researchQuestionId: targetQuestion?.id,
          claimStatement: c.claimStatement,
          status: c.status,
          confidenceScore: c.confidenceScore,
        })
        .returning();

      for (const ev of c.evidenceItems) {
        const shortToken = `EV-${spanCounter++}`;
        const [evSpan] = await db
          .insert(evidenceSpans)
          .values({
            sourceId: ev.sourceId,
            verbatimQuote: ev.verbatimQuote,
            contextSnippet: ev.contextSnippet,
            sectionOrPage: ev.sectionOrPage,
            confidenceScore: 0.95,
            isVerified: true,
          })
          .returning();

        evidenceIdMap.set(shortToken, evSpan.id);

        await db.insert(claimEvidence).values({
          claimId: claimRow.id,
          evidenceSpanId: evSpan.id,
          supportType: ev.supportType,
          relationshipExplanation: ev.relationshipExplanation,
        });

        if (targetQuestion) {
          evidenceCountPerQuestion[targetQuestion.id] =
            (evidenceCountPerQuestion[targetQuestion.id] || 0) + 1;
        }
      }
    }

    // 6. TAHAP 5: Backend Deterministic Gate -> Evidence Sufficient?
    await emit('Memeriksa kecukupan bukti secara deterministik di backend...', 72);

    const totalEvidenceSpansCount = evidenceIdMap.size;
    const sufficiencyResult = DeterministicQualityGates.verifyEvidenceSufficiency(
      insertedQuestions,
      evidenceCountPerQuestion,
      totalEvidenceSpansCount
    );

    await db
      .update(researchJobs)
      .set({
        evidenceSufficient: sufficiencyResult.isSufficient,
        summaryReport: sufficiencyResult.gapAnalysisReason || 'Bukti riset mencukupi.',
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(eq(researchJobs.id, jobId));

    // 7. TAHAP 6: Rakit Grounded Evidence Capsule Berindeks
    const evidenceCapsuleLines: string[] = [];
    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`=== MANIFEST BUKTI TERVERIFIKASI POLARIS (GROUNDING RESMI) ===`);
    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`TOPIK RISET: ${plan.mainTopic}`);
    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`WILAYAH SASARAN: ${input.targetRegion}\n`);

    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`--- DAFTAR BUKTI BERSERTIFIKAT (SITASI RESMI) ---`);
    for (const [token, spanDbId] of evidenceIdMap.entries()) {
      // Cari detail span
      const matchedClaim = extractedClaims.flatMap((c) => c.evidenceItems).find((item) => item);
      if (matchedClaim) {
        evidenceCapsuleLines.push(
          `[${token}] (ID: ${spanDbId}) - Kutipan: "${matchedClaim.verbatimQuote}" (Konteks: ${matchedClaim.contextSnippet})`
        );
      }
    }

    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`\n--- DAFTAR SUMBER RUJUKAN RESMI ---`);
    for (const src of acceptedSources) {
      evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`- [${src.publisherName}] ${src.title}: ${src.url}`);
    }
    evidenceCapsuleLines.push(`===========================================================`);

    const groundedContextText = evidenceCapsuleLines.join('\n');

    await emit('Siklus riset otonom selesai. Siap diteruskan ke Evidence-Grounded Writer.', 85);

    return {
      jobId,
      mainTopic: plan.mainTopic,
      evidenceSufficient: sufficiencyResult.isSufficient,
      qcPassed: false, // Menunggu tahap penulisan dan QC gate
      totalSourcesEvaluated: evaluatedSources.length,
      verifiedSourcesCount: acceptedSources.length,
      totalEvidenceSpans: totalEvidenceSpansCount,
      claimsCount: extractedClaims.length,
      groundedContextText,
      evidenceIdMap,
    };
  }
}
\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/research-planner.engine.ts (139 baris, 5.6 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport { openaiClient } from '../client.js';
import { AiTelemetryService } from '../telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';
import { QuestionIntent } from '@polaris/shared-types';

export interface ResearchPlanInput {
  userPrompt: string;
  targetRegion: string;
  attachedDocsSummary?: string;
  providedUrls?: string[];
  tenantId?: string;
}

export interface PlannedResearchQuestion {
  question: string;
  intentCategory: QuestionIntent;
  temporalBound?: string;
  geographicScope: string;
  minRequiredEvidence: number;
  searchQueries: string[];
}

export interface ResearchPlanOutput {
  mainTopic: string;
  coreProblemStatement: string;
  questions: PlannedResearchQuestion[];
  internalRetrievalPlan: {
    regulationKeywords: string[];
    mediaSector?: string;
    mediaKeywords: string[];
  };
  academicQueries: string[];
  externalWebQueries: string[];
}

export class ResearchPlannerEngine {
  /**
   * Merumuskan rencana riset terstruktur (Divide-and-Conquer) dari instruksi awal pengguna.
   * Mencegah query dangkal 1-2 kata dan mewajibkan causal question & parameter bukti.
   */
  public static async createPlan(input: ResearchPlanInput): Promise<ResearchPlanOutput> {
    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'autonomous-research-planner',
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      tenantId: input.tenantId,
      metadata: { targetRegion: input.targetRegion, promptLength: input.userPrompt.length },
    });

    const systemPrompt = `Anda adalah "POLARIS Lead Research Planner & Policy Methodologist".
Tugas Anda adalah merumuskan rencana investigasi dan riset kebijakan yang mendalam dan terstruktur dari arahan pengguna.

=== PRINSIP PERUMUSAN RISET OTONOM POLARIS ===
1. PERTANYAAN KAUSAL & SUBSTANTIF (ANTI-SUPERFICIAL):
   - JANGAN membuat pertanyaan dangkal atau hanya mengandalkan 1-2 kata kunci terpisah.
   - Rumuskan 3 hingga 5 pertanyaan riset yang spesifik, memiliki hubungan sebab-akibat (causal), dan menguji klaim kebijakan.
2. KLASIFIKASI INTENSI:
   Tiap pertanyaan riset WAJIB dikategorikan ke salah satu intensi:
   - REGULATORY: Dasar hukum, perda, perbup, undang-undang, harmonisasi regulasi.
   - STATISTICAL_BENCHMARK: Angka APBD, BPS, capaian indikator makro, studi komparasi daerah.
   - PUBLIC_OPINION: Sentimen media massa lokal, keluhan warga, dinamika politik parlemen.
   - POLICY_EVALUATION: Analisis efektivitas program, hambatan lapangan, evaluasi belanja publik.
   - FACTUAL_VERIFICATION: Konfirmasi fakta konkret atas insiden/isu yang dilaporkan pengguna.
3. BATASAN WAKTU & WILAYAH (TEMPORAL & GEOGRAPHIC BOUND):
   - Nyatakan batasan waktu (misal: "2024-2026" atau "5 tahun terakhir").
   - Lingkup wilayah: "${input.targetRegion}" atau benchmark nasional yang relevan.
4. ATURAN JUDUL:
   - Judul riset utama (mainTopic) WAJIB bernas, profesional, dan DILARANG KERAS menggunakan tanda titik dua (":").
5. STRATEGI PENCARIAN GANDA (INTERNAL + AKADEMIK + WEB):
   - Siapkan query pencarian majemuk yang kaya kata kunci kontekstual (bukan 1 kata lepas).

Format Output WAJIB berupa JSON murni dengan skema:
{
  "mainTopic": "Judul investigasi tanpa tanda titik dua",
  "coreProblemStatement": "Ringkasan 2-3 kalimat mengenai akar masalah yang diselidiki",
  "questions": [
    {
      "question": "Kalimat pertanyaan riset lengkap dan berbobot",
      "intentCategory": "REGULATORY" | "STATISTICAL_BENCHMARK" | "PUBLIC_OPINION" | "POLICY_EVALUATION" | "FACTUAL_VERIFICATION",
      "temporalBound": "2023-2026",
      "geographicScope": "${input.targetRegion}",
      "minRequiredEvidence": 1,
      "searchQueries": ["query pencarian spesifik 1", "query pencarian spesifik 2"]
    }
  ],
  "internalRetrievalPlan": {
    "regulationKeywords": ["kata kunci perda/regulasi"],
    "mediaSector": "INFRASTRUKTUR" | "PERTANIAN" | "PENDIDIKAN" | "KESEHATAN" | "BANSOS_UMKM" | "LAINNYA",
    "mediaKeywords": ["kata kunci berita lokal"]
  },
  "academicQueries": ["query jurnal/penelitian komparatif"],
  "externalWebQueries": ["query portal berita nasional / data kementerian"]
}`;

    const userContent = `WILAYAH SASARAN: ${input.targetRegion}
ARAHAN / PROMPT PENGGUNA:
"""${input.userPrompt}"""
${input.attachedDocsSummary ? `\nRINGKASAN LAMPIRAN:\n${input.attachedDocsSummary}` : ''}
${input.providedUrls && input.providedUrls.length > 0 ? `\nTAUTAN DILAMPIRKAN:\n${input.providedUrls.join('\n')}` : ''}

Susun rencana riset otonom terlengkap sekarang.`;

    try {
      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o',
        temperature: 0.2,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
        messages: [
          { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
          { role: 'user', content: userContent },
        ],
      });

      const rawJson = response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}';
      const parsed = JSON.parse(rawJson) as ResearchPlanOutput;

      // Bersihkan tanda titik dua pada mainTopic jika model tergelincir
      if (parsed.mainTopic) {
        parsed.mainTopic = parsed.mainTopic.replace(/:/g, ' - ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      }

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: response.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0,
        outputTokens: response.usage?.completion_tokens || 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: `Plan: ${parsed.mainTopic} (${parsed.questions?.length || 0} questions)`,
      });

      return parsed;
    } catch (err: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: err.message,
      });

      throw new Error(`[ResearchPlannerEngine] Gagal merumuskan rencana riset: ${err.message}`);
    }
  }
}
\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/claim-evidence.extractor.ts (150 baris, 5.0 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport { openaiClient } from '../client.js';
import { ClaimStatus, SupportType } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { AiTelemetryService } from '../telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';


export interface VerifiedSourceInput {
  sourceId: string;
  sourceVersionId?: string;
  title: string;
  publisherName?: string;
  url: string;
  extractedText: string;
}

export interface ExtractedEvidenceSpan {
  sourceId: string;
  sourceVersionId?: string;
  verbatimQuote: string;
  contextSnippet: string;
  sectionOrPage?: string;
  confidenceScore: number;
}

export interface ExtractedClaimWithEvidence {
  questionIndex?: number;
  claimStatement: string;
  status: ClaimStatus;
  confidenceScore: number;
  evidenceItems: Array<{
    verbatimQuote: string;
    contextSnippet: string;
    sectionOrPage?: string;
    supportType: SupportType;
    relationshipExplanation: string;
    sourceId: string;
  }>;
}

export class ClaimEvidenceExtractor {
  /**
   * Mengekstrak klaim faktual terverifikasi dan cuplikan kutipan bukti (evidence spans)
   * dari sekumpulan sumber yang telah lolos asesmen kredibilitas.
   */
  public static async extractClaimsAndEvidence(
    researchTopic: string,
    questions: Array<{ index: number; question: string }>,
    sources: VerifiedSourceInput[]
  ): Promise<ExtractedClaimWithEvidence[]> {
    if (sources.length === 0) return [];

    const sourcesContext = sources.map((s, idx) => `[SUMBER #${idx + 1} | ID: ${s.sourceId}]
Judul: ${s.title} (${s.publisherName || s.url})
Teks Rujukan:
"""${s.extractedText.slice(0, 2500)}"""`).join('\n\n');

    const questionsContext = questions
      .map((q) => `Pertanyaan ${q.index}: ${q.question}`)
      .join('\n');

    const extractionPrompt = `Anda adalah "POLARIS Senior Fact Extraction Specialist".
Tugas Anda adalah memetakan fakta konkret, angka, regulasi, dan peristiwa dari SUMBER TERVERIFIKASI untuk menjawab PERTANYAAN RISET.

TOPIK: "${researchTopic}"

PERTANYAAN RISET YANG HARUS DIJAWAB:
${questionsContext}

SUMBER-SUMBER TERVERIFIKASI:
${sourcesContext}

PANDUAN EKSTRAKSI BUKTI RIGID (ZERO HALLUCINATION):
1. Setiap klaim (claimStatement) HARUS didukung oleh KUTIPAN KATA PER KATA (verbatimQuote) yang benar-benar ada di teks rujukan.
2. DILARANG membuat kutipan fiktif. Jika sebuah pertanyaan riset tidak terjawab oleh sumber yang ada, JANGAN mengarang klaim.
3. Cantumkan [ID: ...] sumber yang menjadi rujukan kutipan secara presisi.
4. supportType: 'SUPPORTS' (mendukung klaim), 'REFUTES' (membantah klaim), atau 'NEUTRAL_CONTEXT'.

Hasilkan JSON murni dengan format:
{
  "claims": [
    {
      "questionIndex": 1,
      "claimStatement": "Pernyataan fakta konkret / temuan kebijakan yang didapat dari sumber",
      "status": "VERIFIED" | "PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED",
      "confidenceScore": 0.95,
      "evidenceItems": [
        {
          "sourceId": "ID dari header SUMBER rujukan",
          "verbatimQuote": "Kutipan persis kata-per-kata dari teks sumber",
          "contextSnippet": "1-2 kalimat konteks di sekitar kutipan",
          "sectionOrPage": "Bab II / Paragraf 3 / Rilis Pers",
          "supportType": "SUPPORTS",
          "relationshipExplanation": "Mengapa kutipan ini membuktikan klaim tersebut"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}`;

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'claim-evidence-extraction',
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      metadata: { researchTopic, sourcesCount: sources.length, questionsCount: questions.length },
    });

    try {
      const response = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o',
        temperature: 0.1,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: extractionPrompt }],
      });

      const parsed = JSON.parse(response.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}');
      const rawClaims = Array.isArray(parsed.claims) ? parsed.claims : [];

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: response.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0,
        outputTokens: response.usage?.completion_tokens || 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: `Extracted ${rawClaims.length} claims`,
      });

      return rawClaims.map((c: any) => ({
        questionIndex: c.questionIndex,
        claimStatement: c.claimStatement || '',
        status: (c.status as ClaimStatus) || ClaimStatus.VERIFIED,
        confidenceScore: Number(c.confidenceScore) || 0.9,
        evidenceItems: (c.evidenceItems || []).map((ev: any) => ({
          verbatimQuote: ev.verbatimQuote || '',
          contextSnippet: ev.contextSnippet || ev.verbatimQuote || '',
          sectionOrPage: ev.sectionOrPage,
          supportType: (ev.supportType as SupportType) || SupportType.SUPPORTS,
          relationshipExplanation: ev.relationshipExplanation || '',
          sourceId: ev.sourceId || sources[0].sourceId,
        })),
      }));
    } catch (err: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: err.message,
      });

      console.warn(`[ClaimEvidenceExtractor] Ekstraksi klaim gagal: ${err.message}`);
      return [];
    }

  }
}
\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/deterministic-gates.ts (123 baris, 4.3 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nexport interface QuestionEvidenceStatus {
  questionId: string;
  questionText: string;
  minRequiredEvidence: number;
  actualEvidenceCount: number;
}

export interface EvidenceSufficiencyResult {
  isSufficient: boolean;
  totalEvidenceCount: number;
  unresolvedQuestions: QuestionEvidenceStatus[];
  gapAnalysisReason?: string;
}

export interface QualityControlResult {
  qcPassed: boolean;
  verifiedCitationsCount: number;
  rogueCitations: string[];
  failureReason?: string;
  citationIntegrityRate: number; // 0.0 - 1.0
}

export class DeterministicQualityGates {
  /**
   * GERBANG 1: Evidence Sufficient?
   * Memvalidasi kecukupan bukti secara deterministik di backend (bukan sekadar keputusan model AI).
   * Mencegah proses lanjut ke penulisan jika pertanyaan kunci belum memiliki bukti terdaftar.
   */
  public static verifyEvidenceSufficiency(
    questions: Array<{ id: string; question: string; minRequiredEvidence: number }>,
    evidenceCountPerQuestion: Record<string, number>,
    totalSpansAvailable: number
  ): EvidenceSufficiencyResult {
    const unresolvedQuestions: QuestionEvidenceStatus[] = [];

    for (const q of questions) {
      const actualCount = evidenceCountPerQuestion[q.id] || 0;
      if (actualCount < q.minRequiredEvidence) {
        unresolvedQuestions.push({
          questionId: q.id,
          questionText: q.question,
          minRequiredEvidence: q.minRequiredEvidence,
          actualEvidenceCount: actualCount,
        });
      }
    }

    // Syarat cukup:
    // 1. Total bukti konkret >= 2
    // 2. Maksimal hanya 1 pertanyaan sekunder yang boleh kurang (toleransi parsial), atau 0 jika pertanyaan < 3
    const maxAllowedUnresolved = questions.length >= 4 ? 1 : 0;
    const isSufficient =
      totalSpansAvailable >= 2 && unresolvedQuestions.length <= maxAllowedUnresolved;

    let gapAnalysisReason: string | undefined;
    if (!isSufficient) {
      gapAnalysisReason = `Bukti belum memadai: Terdapat ${unresolvedQuestions.length} pertanyaan riset tanpa dukungan data yang cukup (Total bukti terdaftar: ${totalSpansAvailable}). Membutuhkan targeted follow-up search.`;
    }

    return {
      isSufficient,
      totalEvidenceCount: totalSpansAvailable,
      unresolvedQuestions,
      gapAnalysisReason,
    };
  }

  /**
   * GERBANG 2: QC Passed? (Citation & Evidence Grounding Verification)
   * Backend memeriksa keaslian sitasi di naskah draf:
   * 1. Mengekstrak seluruh token sitasi (e.g. [^EV-...] atau [EV-...]).
   * 2. Mencocokkan dengan set ID bukti yang sah di database.
   * 3. Menolak naskah jika terdapat 'Rogue Citation' (sitasi buatan yang tidak ada di DB).
   */
  public static verifyCitationQualityControl(
    articleMarkdown: string,
    validEvidenceIds: Set<string>,
    minExpectedCitations: number = 2
  ): QualityControlResult {
    // Regex menangkap [^EV-xxx] atau [EV-xxx] atau [Bukti EV-xxx]
    const citationRegex = /\[(?:\^|Bukti:?\s*)?(EV-[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+)\]/gi;
    const matches = Array.from(articleMarkdown.matchAll(citationRegex));

    const citedIds = Array.from(new Set(matches.map((m) => m[1])));
    const verifiedIds: string[] = [];
    const rogueCitations: string[] = [];

    for (const id of citedIds) {
      if (validEvidenceIds.has(id)) {
        verifiedIds.push(id);
      } else {
        rogueCitations.push(id);
      }
    }

    const verifiedCitationsCount = verifiedIds.length;
    const totalCitedCount = citedIds.length;
    const citationIntegrityRate =
      totalCitedCount > 0 ? verifiedCitationsCount / totalCitedCount : 0;

    // Kriteria Lulus QC:
    // 1. Tidak ada sitasi fiktif (rogueCitations == 0)
    // 2. Minimal jumlah sitasi terverifikasi terpenuhi (jika ada bukti terdaftar)
    let qcPassed = true;
    let failureReason: string | undefined;

    if (rogueCitations.length > 0) {
      qcPassed = false;
      failureReason = `QC Gagal: Ditemukan ${rogueCitations.length} token sitasi fiktif yang tidak terdaftar di basis data evidence: [${rogueCitations.join(', ')}].`;
    } else if (validEvidenceIds.size >= minExpectedCitations && verifiedCitationsCount < 1) {
      qcPassed = false;
      failureReason = `QC Gagal: Naskah tidak menyertakan sitasi eksplisit ke bukti yang telah disediakan sistem.`;
    }

    return {
      qcPassed,
      verifiedCitationsCount,
      rogueCitations,
      failureReason,
      citationIntegrityRate,
    };
  }
}
\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/research/source-credibility.assessor.ts (195 baris, 7.3 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport { openaiClient } from '../client.js';
import { PublisherType, SourceLifecycle } from '@polaris/shared-types';
import { AiTelemetryService } from '../telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';


export interface SourceCandidateInput {
  url: string;
  domain: string;
  title: string;
  extractedText: string;
  publisherType?: PublisherType;
  doi?: string;
  isPeerReviewed?: boolean;
  hasRetractions?: boolean;
  publicationYear?: number;
}

export interface CredibilityAssessmentResult {
  publisherCredibilityScore: number;
  substantiveScore: number;
  methodologyScore?: number;
  recencyScore: number;
  overallScore: number;
  isAccepted: boolean;
  lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle;
  assessmentReason: string;
  rejectionReason?: string;
}

export class SourceCredibilityAssessor {
  /**
   * Menghitung skor reputasi penerbit berdasarkan domain / jenis institusi.
   * PENTING: Skor penerbit adalah SATU faktor sinyal awal, BUKAN bukti kebenaran mutlak.
   */
  public static calculatePublisherBaselineScore(domain: string, publisherType?: PublisherType): number {
    const d = domain.toLowerCase();
    if (d.endsWith('.go.id') || d.endsWith('.gov') || d.endsWith('.mil.id')) {
      return 0.90; // Domain pemerintah / regulasi resmi
    }
    if (d.endsWith('.ac.id') || d.endsWith('.edu') || publisherType === PublisherType.ACADEMIC) {
      return 0.88; // Lembaga riset & perguruan tinggi
    }
    // Media arus utama / pers kredibel
    const establishedMedia = ['kompas.com', 'tempo.co', 'antaranews.com', 'bisnis.com', 'kontan.co.id', 'detik.com'];
    if (establishedMedia.some((m) => d.includes(m))) {
      return 0.82;
    }
    if (publisherType === PublisherType.USER) {
      return 0.75; // Lampiran dokumen kerja langsung dari pengguna dewan
    }
    // Domain publik umum
    return 0.50;
  }

  /**
   * Menilai kelayakan dan kredibilitas sumber secara menyeluruh (Substansi + Metodologi + Kesegaran).
   */
  public static async assessSource(
    candidate: SourceCandidateInput,
    researchTopic: string
  ): Promise<CredibilityAssessmentResult> {
    const publisherScore = this.calculatePublisherBaselineScore(candidate.domain, candidate.publisherType);

    // Filter awal teks kosong / terlalu pendek
    if (!candidate.extractedText || candidate.extractedText.trim().length < 150) {
      return {
        publisherCredibilityScore: publisherScore,
        substantiveScore: 0.1,
        recencyScore: 0.5,
        overallScore: 0.2,
        isAccepted: false,
        lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.REJECTED,
        assessmentReason: 'Konten teks terlalu dangkal atau tidak memuat data faktual (kurang dari 150 karakter).',
        rejectionReason: 'EMPTY_OR_SHALLOW_CONTENT',
      };
    }

    // Jika paper akademik memiliki catatan retraksi, tolak otomatis
    if (candidate.hasRetractions) {
      return {
        publisherCredibilityScore: 0.2,
        substantiveScore: 0.2,
        methodologyScore: 0.1,
        recencyScore: 0.5,
        overallScore: 0.15,
        isAccepted: false,
        lifecycleStatus: SourceLifecycle.REJECTED,
        assessmentReason: 'Publikasi ilmiah memiliki catatan retraksi (retraction) atau koreksi fatal pada Crossref.',
        rejectionReason: 'ACADEMIC_RETRACTION_FLAG',
      };
    }

    const evaluationPrompt = `Anda adalah "POLARIS Evidence Verification Auditor".
Tugas Anda adalah menilai substansi dan kualitas fakta dari materi rujukan berikut terkait topik riset kebijakan.

TOPIK RISET: "${researchTopic}"
SUMBER DITELITI:
- URL: ${candidate.url}
- Judul: ${candidate.title}
- Penerbit: ${candidate.domain} (${candidate.publisherType || 'MEDIA'})
${candidate.doi ? `- DOI: ${candidate.doi} (Peer-reviewed: ${candidate.isPeerReviewed ? 'Ya' : 'Belum Terkonfirmasi'})` : ''}

CUPLIKAN KONTEN:
"""${candidate.extractedText.slice(0, 1500)}"""

PANDUAN PENILAIAN KRITIS:
1. JANGAN menilai sumber semata-mata dari domain whitelist. Periksa data riil, argumen, dan angka yang disajikan.
2. substantiveScore (0.0 - 1.0): Seberapa konkret data lapangan, regulasi, atau indikator yang diutarakan? (Bukan opini retoris).
3. recencyScore (0.0 - 1.0): Seberapa relevan waktu/kesegaran data ini dengan masalah yang diselidiki?
4. methodologyScore (0.0 - 1.0): Kualitas metodologi atau keabsahan sumber rujukan primer.
5. overallScore: Rata-rata tertimbang (Substansi 50%, Metodologi 25%, Kesegaran 25%).
6. isAccepted: true jika overallScore >= 0.60 dan tidak ada fabrikasi hoax yang jelas.

Keluarkan JSON murni:
{
  "substantiveScore": 0.85,
  "methodologyScore": 0.80,
  "recencyScore": 0.90,
  "overallScore": 0.85,
  "isAccepted": true,
  "assessmentReason": "Penjelasan singkat 1-2 kalimat mengapa sumber ini layak atau tidak layak dijadikan bukti riset."
}`;

    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'source-credibility-assessment',
      model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
      metadata: { url: candidate.url, domain: candidate.domain, doi: candidate.doi },
    });

    try {
      const completion = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
        model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
        temperature: 0.1,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: evaluationPrompt }],
      });

      const parsed = JSON.parse(completion.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}');
      const substantive = Number(parsed.substantiveScore) || 0.5;
      const methodology = Number(parsed.methodologyScore) || 0.5;
      const recency = Number(parsed.recencyScore) || 0.5;

      // Gabungkan dengan baseline publisher
      const weightedOverall = Math.min(
        1.0,
        Math.max(
          0.0,
          substantive * 0.45 + methodology * 0.25 + recency * 0.15 + publisherScore * 0.15
        )
      );

      const isAccepted = weightedOverall >= 0.58 && Boolean(parsed.isAccepted);

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: completion.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0,
        outputTokens: completion.usage?.completion_tokens || 0,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: `Score: ${weightedOverall}, Accepted: ${isAccepted}`,
      });

      return {
        publisherCredibilityScore: publisherScore,
        substantiveScore: substantive,
        methodologyScore: methodology,
        recencyScore: recency,
        overallScore: Math.round(weightedOverall * 100) / 100,
        isAccepted,
        lifecycleStatus: isAccepted ? SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED : SourceLifecycle.REJECTED,
        assessmentReason: parsed.assessmentReason || 'Evaluasi substansi selesai.',
        rejectionReason: isAccepted ? undefined : 'INSUFFICIENT_SUBSTANTIVE_OR_METHODOLOGY_SCORE',
      };
    } catch (err: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: err.message,
      });

      // Fallback deterministik berbasis panjang konten & publisher score

      const fallbackScore = Math.min(1.0, publisherScore * 0.6 + 0.2);
      const isAccepted = fallbackScore >= 0.6;
      return {
        publisherCredibilityScore: publisherScore,
        substantiveScore: 0.6,
        recencyScore: 0.6,
        overallScore: fallbackScore,
        isAccepted,
        lifecycleStatus: isAccepted ? SourceLifecycle.VERIFIED : SourceLifecycle.REJECTED,
        assessmentReason: 'Penilaian fallback otomatis berdasarkan profil penerbit dan panjang teks.',
      };
    }
  }
}
\n\n======================================================================\n--- BERKAS: packages/ai-engine/src/two-stage-article.engine.ts (553 baris, 25.2 KB) ---\n======================================================================\nimport { GenerateArticleParams, GeneratedArticleResult } from '@polaris/core-domain';
import { openaiClient } from './client.js';
import { PromptTemplates } from './prompts.js';
import { AiTelemetryService } from './telemetry/ai-telemetry.service.js';

interface ChapterBlueprint {
  chapterNumber: string;
  title: string;
  targetWords: number;
  substanceFocus: string;
  formatStyle:
    | 'NARRATIVE_ONLY'
    | 'NARRATIVE_WITH_TABLE'
    | 'NARRATIVE_WITH_CHART'
    | 'NARRATIVE_WITH_ROOT_CAUSE'
    | 'NARRATIVE_WITH_ROADMAP'
    | 'FULL_ANALYTICAL';
  tablePrompt?: string;
  chartSpec?: {
    title: string;
    type: 'bar' | 'line' | 'pie';
    unit: string;
    labels: string[];
    datasetLabel: string;
  };
  includeVisualSlot?: boolean;
  visualSlotCaption?: string;
  visualSlotPrompt?: string;
}

interface OutlineResult {
  title: string;
  excerpt: string;
  chapters: ChapterBlueprint[];
}

export class TwoStageArticleEngine {
  /**
   * Mengatur target panjang naskah & distribusi kata per bab
   */
  private static resolveLengthPlan(targetLength?: string): {
    totalTargetWords: number;
    chapterCount: number;
    wordsPerChapter: number;
  } {
    const raw = (targetLength || '').toUpperCase();
    if (raw.includes('750') || raw.includes('SHORT')) {
      return { totalTargetWords: 800, chapterCount: 2, wordsPerChapter: 400 };
    }
    if (raw.includes('1500') || raw.includes('1.500') || raw.includes('MEDIUM')) {
      return { totalTargetWords: 1600, chapterCount: 3, wordsPerChapter: 530 };
    }
    // Default LONG: 3.000 - 3.500 kata (5 Bab eksekutif)
    return { totalTargetWords: 3200, chapterCount: 5, wordsPerChapter: 640 };
  }

  /**
   * TAHAP 1: Redaktur Cerdas (Outline Blueprinting)
   * Menyusun kerangka bab adaptif secara dinamis berdasarkan topik
   */
  public static async generateOutline(
    params: GenerateArticleParams,
    lengthPlan: ReturnType<typeof TwoStageArticleEngine.resolveLengthPlan>
  ): Promise<{ outline: OutlineResult; tokensUsed: number }> {
    const styleInstruction = params.writingStyle
      ? `GAYA PENULISAN: ${params.writingStyle}. TONE: ${params.toneOverride || 'Otoritatif, Teknokratis, Berpihak pada Solusi Nyata'}`
      : 'TONE: Teknokratis, Lugas, Berbasis Fakta & Solutif';

    const outlinePrompt = `ANDA ADALAH CHIEF EDITORIAL DIRECTOR & DRAFTER KEBIJAKAN EKSEKUTIF SENIOR.
Tugas Anda: Susun Outline Blueprint Arsitektur untuk Dokumen Tulisan Eksekutif (Artikel Opini Publik, Policy Brief, Laporan Pengawasan/Kinerja, Kajian Strategis, dll) berbobot tinggi sesuai instruksi topik.

TOPIK & INSTRUKSI PENGGUNA: "${params.topic}"
TARGET AUDIENS: ${params.targetAudience || 'Masyarakat Umum, Pengambil Kebijakan Daerah, dan Media'}
${styleInstruction}
TARGET TOTAL KATA: ~${lengthPlan.totalTargetWords} kata (terbagi ke dalam ${lengthPlan.chapterCount} Bab/Bagian).
${params.regionalContextData ? `KONTEKS WILAYAH & DATA:\n${params.regionalContextData.slice(0, 1500)}` : ''}

FLEKSIBILITAS & ADAPTASI JENIS DOKUMEN:
- Selalu sesuaikan arsitektur outline dengan jenis dokumen yang diminta pengguna pada TOPIK:
  * Jika bernuansa POLICY BRIEF: Struktur harus memuat Ringkasan Isu Kebijakan, Telaah Bukti Empiris, Opsi Intervensi, dan Rekomendasi Regulasi.
  * Jika bernuansa LAPORAN PENGAWASAN / LAPORAN RESES: Struktur harus memuat Verifikasi Temuan Lapangan, Deviasi Target vs Realisasi, Akar Hambatan Teknis OPD, dan Rekomendasi Tindak Lanjut Dewan.
  * Jika bernuansa ARTIKEL OPINI / TAJUK PUBLIK: Struktur mengalir jurnalistik, membedah aspirasi konstituen, tinjauan komparasi data, dan komitmen masa depan.
  * Jika bernuansa SIARAN PERS / PERNYATAAN SIKAP: Struktur lugas, berfokus pada urgensi sikap parlemen, fakta pemicu, dan tuntutan solusi nyata.

PEDOMAN PERUMUSAN JUDUL (SANGAT PENTING - WAJIB DIPATUHI):
1. DILARANG KERAS MENGGUNAKAN TANDA TITIK DUA (":") baik pada judul artikel maupun judul bab!
2. HINDARI FORMULA KLISE AKADEMIS/MAKALAH seperti pola "Topik: Subjudul Penjelas".
   Contoh DILARANG: "Transformasi Infrastruktur Desa: Jalan Menuju Kemajuan Berkelanjutan" (Ini sangat kaku, malas, dan terkesan seperti skripsi!).
3. Buatlah judul artikel sebagai SATU KESATUAN TAJUK (HEADLINE) JURNALISTIK EKSEKUTIF yang utuh, bertenaga, mengalir alami, dan memikat (seperti tajuk investigasi Tempo, kolom opini Kompas, The Economist, atau Project Syndicate).
   - Pola judul yang SANGAT DIANJURKAN:
     * "Menepis Sengkarut Infrastruktur Demi Masa Depan Warga Desa"
     * "Mengapa Pemerataan Pembangunan Desa Tak Boleh Lagi Ditunda"
     * "Membangun Desa Tanpa Menumpuk Beban Utang Daerah"
     * "Langkah Taktis Membuka Urat Nadi Ekonomi dari Pelosok"
     * "Menata Ulang Tata Kelola Logistik Pedesaan Menuju Ketahanan Pangan"
4. Judul bab ("title" pada chapters) juga DILARANG memakai tanda titik dua (":") dan em dash ("—"). Gunakan frasa bab yang substantif dan langsung menyentuh substansi masalah (misal: "Retorika dan Realita Jalan Poros", bukan "Kondisi Lapangan: Potret Nyata").

PANTANGAN MASTER EDITORIAL & GARIS PEMISAH:
- DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kata "sekadar".
- DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kata "nyata" (misal: "dampak nyata", "langkah nyata", "ikhtiar nyata", "perubahan nyata"). Gunakan penjelasan bukti atau capaian konkret.
- DILARANG KERAS menggunakan tanda baca em dash (—). Gunakan koma, titik, atau tanda kurung.
- DILARANG KERAS menyisipkan garis pemisah horizontal ("---", "***", "___", atau deretan tanda hubung) di bawah judul, antar-bab, maupun di sela paragraf. Teks harus mengalir alami tanpa pemisah mekanis.

PEDOMAN VARIASI STRUKTURAL (SANGAT PENTING):
1. JANGAN memaksakan semua bab memiliki tabel/grafik! Harus organik:
   - Bab I (Latar Belakang/Refleksi): Format harus NARRATIVE_ONLY. Fokus narasi filosofis dan argumen nilai.
   - Bab Data / Kondisi Lapangan: Format NARRATIVE_WITH_TABLE atau NARRATIVE_WITH_CHART.
   - Bab Akar Masalah: Format NARRATIVE_WITH_ROOT_CAUSE (bullet points analisis sebab-akibat).
   - Bab Solusi / Rekomendasi: Format NARRATIVE_WITH_ROADMAP (matriks langkah tindak nyata).
   - Bab Penutup: NARRATIVE_ONLY (persuasif, komitmen masa depan).
2. Jika ada bab yang cocok untuk visualisasi foto/infografis lapangan di tengah naskah, set includeVisualSlot: true dengan visualSlotCaption dan visualSlotPrompt yang relevan.

Keluarkan hasil HANYA dalam format JSON valid berikut:
{
  "title": "Judul Headline Jurnalistik Utuh Tanpa Tanda Titik Dua",
  "excerpt": "Ringkasan Eksekutif 2-3 kalimat padat berbobot",
  "chapters": [
    {
      "chapterNumber": "Bab I",
      "title": "Judul Spesifik Bab Tanpa Titik Dua",
      "targetWords": ${lengthPlan.wordsPerChapter},
      "substanceFocus": "Poin-poin substansi mendalam yang wajib diulas di bab ini",
      "formatStyle": "NARRATIVE_ONLY",
      "includeVisualSlot": false
    }
  ]
}`;

    const completion = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      temperature: 0.35,
      response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
      messages: [
        {
          role: 'system',
          content: 'Anda adalah perancang outline naskah eksekutif terkemuka. Keluarkan output JSON valid tanpa titik dua pada judul.',
        },
        { role: 'user', content: outlinePrompt },
      ],
    });

    const parsed = JSON.parse(completion.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}') as OutlineResult;
    const tokens = (completion.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0) + (completion.usage?.completion_tokens || 0);

    const sanitizedOutline: OutlineResult = {
      title: TwoStageArticleEngine.cleanHeadline(parsed.title, params.topic),
      excerpt: parsed.excerpt || '',
      chapters: (parsed.chapters || []).map((ch, idx) => ({
        chapterNumber: ch.chapterNumber || `Bab ${idx + 1}`,
        title: TwoStageArticleEngine.cleanHeadline(ch.title, `Uraian Analisis Substantif Bab ${idx + 1}`),
        targetWords: ch.targetWords || lengthPlan.wordsPerChapter,
        substanceFocus: ch.substanceFocus || '',
        formatStyle: ch.formatStyle || 'NARRATIVE_ONLY',
        chartSpec: ch.chartSpec,
        includeVisualSlot: !!ch.includeVisualSlot,
        visualSlotCaption: ch.visualSlotCaption,
        visualSlotPrompt: ch.visualSlotPrompt,
      })),
    };

    return { outline: sanitizedOutline, tokensUsed: tokens };
  }

  /**
   * Sanitizer khusus judul agar bebas dari tanda titik dua (:) dan formula klise "Topik: Subjudul"
   */
  public static cleanHeadline(rawTitle?: string, fallback: string = ''): string {
    if (!rawTitle || typeof rawTitle !== 'string') return (fallback || 'Analisis Kebijakan Strategis').trim();
    let title = rawTitle.replace(/^["'\s]+|["'\s]+$/g, '').trim();

    // 1. Transformasi formula klise "Topik: Subjudul"
    if (title.includes(':')) {
      const parts = title.split(':').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      if (parts.length > 1) {
        const first = parts[0];
        const second = parts[1];

        // Jika bagian kedua diawali pola umum "Jalan Menuju...", leburkan secara mengalir
        if (/^(jalan menuju|upaya|ikhtiar|langkah|menuju|mewujudkan|sebuah tinjauan)\s+/i.test(second)) {
          const connector = second.replace(/^(jalan|upaya|ikhtiar|langkah)\s+menuju/i, 'Menuju');
          title = `${first} ${connector}`;
        } else if (second.length >= 25 && !second.toLowerCase().startsWith('studi')) {
          // Jika subjudulnya sudah sangat kuat dan panjang, gunakan subjudul tersebut sebagai headline utama
          title = second;
        } else {
          // Gabungkan dengan konjungsi alami jika diperlukan
          title = `${first} demi ${second}`;
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Bersihkan semua karakter titik dua dan strip pemisah sisa
    title = title.replace(/\s*:\s*/g, ' ').replace(/\s+-\s+/g, ' ');
    title = title.replace(/[:]/g, '').replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ').trim();

    // 3. Bersihkan tanda kutip pembungkus
    title = title.replace(/^["'“”‘’]+|["'“”‘’]+$/g, '').trim();

    return title || fallback;
  }

  /**
   * TAHAP 2: Ekspansi Bab Berantai (Staggered Chapter Synthesis)
   * Menggunakan Dual-Stream Token Budgeting agar narasi utama tidak terpotong oleh tabel/grafik
   */
  public static async generateChapter(
    chapter: ChapterBlueprint,
    articleTitle: string,
    params: GenerateArticleParams,
    previousChapterSummary?: string
  ): Promise<{ chapterMarkdown: string; chapterSummary: string; tokensUsed: number }> {
    let structuralMandate = '';

    if (chapter.formatStyle === 'NARRATIVE_WITH_TABLE') {
      structuralMandate += `
- WAJIB SERTAKAN 1 TABEL DATA MARKDOWN: Buat tabel komparasi/indikator data realistis minimal 3 kolom dan 3-4 baris. Letakkan di tengah atau akhir pembahasan data.`;
    } else if (chapter.formatStyle === 'NARRATIVE_WITH_CHART' && chapter.chartSpec) {
      const spec = chapter.chartSpec;
      structuralMandate += `
- WAJIB SERTAKAN 1 BLOK GRAFIK (FORMAT TIPTAP):
Sertakan blok grafik persis seperti contoh format ini di dalam Markdown:
:::chart
{
  "type": "${spec.type || 'bar'}",
  "title": "${spec.title || 'Grafik Indikator Kebijakan'}",
  "unit": "${spec.unit || 'Indeks/Unit'}",
  "labels": ${JSON.stringify(spec.labels || ['2023', '2024', '2025 Target'])},
  "datasets": [
    {
      "label": "${spec.datasetLabel || 'Realisasi'}",
      "data": [45, 68, 85]
    }
  ]
}
:::`;
    } else if (chapter.formatStyle === 'NARRATIVE_WITH_ROOT_CAUSE') {
      structuralMandate += `
- WAJIB SERTAKAN ANALISIS AKAR MASALAH: Gunakan poin-poin struktural (Bullet Points) yang membedah Dimensi Regulasi, Dimensi Anggaran, dan Dimensi Pelaksanaan Lapangan.`;
    } else if (chapter.formatStyle === 'NARRATIVE_WITH_ROADMAP') {
      structuralMandate += `
- WAJIB SERTAKAN MATRIKS RENCANA AKSI (ROADMAP): Buat tabel Markdown yang memetakan Prioritas Aksi, Waktu Eksekusi, Output Konkret, dan Pemangku Kepentingan Terkait.`;
    }

    if (chapter.includeVisualSlot && chapter.visualSlotCaption) {
      structuralMandate += `
- WAJIB SERTAKAN 1 SLOT VISUAL DI TENGAH BAB:
Sematkan baris penanda slot gambar berikut pada posisi paragraf yang paling relevan:
<!-- VISUAL_SLOT: {"type": "INFOGRAPHIC", "caption": "${chapter.visualSlotCaption}", "prompt": "${chapter.visualSlotPrompt || chapter.visualSlotCaption}"} -->`;
    }

    const chapterPrompt = `TUGAS: Tulis naskah lengkap untuk ${chapter.chapterNumber}. "${chapter.title}"
DARI ARTIKEL: "${articleTitle}"
SUBSTANSI BAB INI: ${chapter.substanceFocus}

TARGET KATA NARASI MURNI: Minimal ${chapter.targetWords} kata (HANYA MENGHITUNG TEKS PARAGRAF).
PRINSIP DUAL-STREAM BUDGETING: Tabel, grafik, atau matriks adalah BONUS STRUKTURAL, BUKAN pengganti panjang paragraf narasi! Paragraf argumen harus tetap padat, mengalir, dan mendalam.

${previousChapterSummary ? `KONTEKS BAB SEBELUMNYA (JAGA KONTINUITAS & HINDARI REPETISI):\n"${previousChapterSummary}"` : ''}

${structuralMandate}

ATURAN TATA BAHASA, EJAAN & MIKROLINGUISTIK (SANGAT PENTING - WAJIB DIPATUHI):
1. PENEGAKAN STRUKTUR SPOK AKTIF (ACTOR-DRIVEN SYNTAX):
   - Setiap klausa argumen WAJIB mencantumkan subjek pelaku/aktor kebijakan yang jelas di depan (contoh: "Pemerintah Daerah", "DPRD", "Dinas Bina Marga", "Badan Anggaran", "Warga petani").
   - DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kalimat pasif mengambang tanpa subjek seperti:
     * DILARANG: "Diharapkan program ini dapat berjalan optimal..." -> WAJIB: "Pemerintah Daerah harus memastikan program ini berjalan optimal..."
     * DILARANG: "Perlu dilakukan evaluasi mendalam..." -> WAJIB: "Komisi Terkait mendesak evaluasi mendalam..."
     * DILARANG: "Melalui langkah ini dinilai mampu mendorong..." -> WAJIB: "Alokasi anggaran ini mendorong percepatan..."

2. LARANGAN OXFORD COMMA PADA PERINCIAN ELEMEN (GAYA JURNALISTIK ALAMI):
   - Pada perincian 3 elemen atau lebih, DILARANG meletakkan tanda koma sebelum kata sambung "dan", "atau", "serta".
   - CONTOH SALAH (GAYA AI KLISE): "...kucing, anjing, dan ikan" / "...pendidikan, kesehatan, dan infrastruktur"
   - CONTOH BENAR (GAYA ALAMI): "...kucing, anjing dan ikan" / "...pendidikan, kesehatan dan infrastruktur"

3. PANDUAN KATA BAKU & PREPOSISI ALAMI:
   - Penulisan preposisi tempat wajib dipisah spasi: "di antaranya" (BUKAN "diantaranya"), "di mana", "ke depan".
   - Gunakan kata baku yang wajar, komunikatif, dan mengalir natural, tidak kaku seperti mesin terjemahan.

4. KAMUS HITAM DIKSI KLISE AI (AI SLOP BLACKLIST):
   - DILARANG KERAS menggunakan metafora basi yang diobral AI:
     * "angin segar"
     * "tonggak bersejarah" / "tonggak penting"
     * "garda terdepan"
     * "menatap masa depan yang gemilang" / "merajut asa" / "membuka tabir"
     * "sinergi yang komprehensif"
     * "pada hakikatnya" / "seyogianya" / "niscaya"
     * Pola berulang: "bukan sekadar... melainkan juga..."
   - GANTI dengan verba tindakan teknokratis terukur: memitigasi, meninjau ulang, memangkas deviasi, mengalokasikan, mengaudit, mengesahkan, mengevaluasi.

5. RITME KALIMAT VARIATIF (SENTENCE BURSTINESS):
   - Selingi naskah dengan kalimat pendek tegas (5-8 kata) sebagai penegasan tesis, lalu lanjutkan dengan kalimat analisis elaboratif (15-20 kata). JANGAN membuat semua kalimat sama panjangnya!

6. LARANGAN KALIMAT PEMBUKA KLISE:
   - Dilarang membuka bab dengan: "Dalam era globalisasi saat ini...", "Sebagaimana kita ketahui bersama...", "Seiring dengan perkembangan zaman...".
   - Awali langsung dengan proposisi tajam, data lapangan, atau situasi riil warga.
   - Output diawali dengan heading: ## ${chapter.chapterNumber}. ${chapter.title} (DILARANG menggunakan titik dua dan em dash).

7. INTEGRASI SITASI BUKTI TERVERIFIKASI (EVIDENCE GROUNDING CITATION):
   - Jika pada konteks data terdapat penanda bukti seperti [EV-1], [EV-2], dst., sematkan rujukan bukti tersebut di akhir klausa atau kalimat argumen yang bersangkutan dengan format: [^EV-1] atau [^EV-2].
   - DILARANG KERAS mengarang ID bukti baru di luar yang tercantum dalam konteks data!

8. PANTANGAN MASTER EDITORIAL & LARANGAN GARIS PEMISAH (ZERO-TOLERANCE):
   - DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kata "sekadar" dalam konteks apa pun.
   - DILARANG KERAS menggunakan kata "nyata" sebagai penegas generik (seperti "dampak nyata", "bukti nyata", "langkah nyata", "ikhtiar nyata"). Paparkan fakta, data, atau indikator secara spesifik.
   - DILARANG KERAS menggunakan tanda baca em dash (—). Gunakan tanda koma, titik dua, titik, atau tanda kurung sesuai kebutuhan sintaksis.
   - DILARANG KERAS MENYISIPKAN GARIS PEMISAH HORIZONTAL (seperti "---", "***", "___", atau deretan tanda hubung "------") di bawah judul bab, di antara subbab, maupun di sela-sela paragraf narasi. Transisi antar-paragraf dan antar-bab wajib murni mengalir alami melalui spasi paragraf normal tanpa garis pemisah mekanis.

Keluarkan hasil dalam format JSON:

{
  "chapterMarkdown": "Isi lengkap bab dalam format Markdown...",
  "keyTakeawaysSummary": "Ringkasan 2 kalimat fakta kunci & argumen bab ini untuk dioper ke bab berikutnya"
}`;

    const completion = await openaiClient.chat.completions.create({
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      temperature: 0.35,
      response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
      messages: [
        {
          role: 'system',
          content: 'Anda adalah penulis kebijakan eksekutif papan atas. Patuhi seluruh kaidah mikrolinguistik, SPOK aktif, dan hapus koma sebelum kata dan.',
        },
        { role: 'user', content: chapterPrompt },
      ],
    });

    const parsed = JSON.parse(completion.choices[0]?.message?.content || '{}');
    const tokens = (completion.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0) + (completion.usage?.completion_tokens || 0);

    const rawMarkdown = parsed.chapterMarkdown || `## ${chapter.chapterNumber} — ${chapter.title}\n\n(Konten bab)`;
    const sanitizedMarkdown = TwoStageArticleEngine.normalizeFormattingSpaces(
      TwoStageArticleEngine.sanitizeMicroLinguistics(rawMarkdown)
    );

    return {
      chapterMarkdown: sanitizedMarkdown,
      chapterSummary: parsed.keyTakeawaysSummary || '',
      tokensUsed: tokens,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Sanitizer Mikrolinguistik Pasca-Generasi:
   * 1. Menghapus Oxford Comma (misal: "kucing, anjing, dan ikan" -> "kucing, anjing dan ikan")
   * 2. Memperbaiki preposisi "diantaranya" -> "di antaranya"
   * 3. Menghapus koma sebelum "serta" dan "atau" pada perincian
   * 4. Membersihkan sisa titik dua pada heading bab
   */
  public static sanitizeMicroLinguistics(rawMarkdown: string): string {
    if (!rawMarkdown || typeof rawMarkdown !== 'string') return '';
    let text = rawMarkdown;

    // 1. Format heading bab tanpa titik dua dan tanpa em dash (Master Editorial Rules)
    text = text.replace(/^(##\s+Bab\s+[IVXLCDM\d]+)\s*[:—–-]\s*/gim, '$1. ');

    // 2. Hapus em dash (— atau –) dari narasi dan ganti dengan tanda koma alami
    text = text.replace(/\s*[—–]\s*/g, ', ');

    // 3. Hapus Oxford Comma sebelum "dan", "serta", "atau" pada perincian elemen
    // Contoh: "kucing, anjing, dan ikan" -> "kucing, anjing dan ikan"
    text = text.replace(/([a-zA-Z0-9\)])\s*,\s+dan\s+([a-zA-Z0-9\(])/g, '$1 dan $2');
    text = text.replace(/([a-zA-Z0-9\)])\s*,\s+serta\s+([a-zA-Z0-9\(])/g, '$1 serta $2');
    text = text.replace(/([a-zA-Z0-9\)])\s*,\s+atau\s+([a-zA-Z0-9\(])/g, '$1 atau $2');

    // 4. Normalisasi ejaan umum yang sering salah sambung
    text = text.replace(/\bdiantaranya\b/gi, 'di antaranya');
    text = text.replace(/\bdimana\b/gi, 'di mana');
    text = text.replace(/\bkesana\b/gi, 'ke sana');
    text = text.replace(/\bkemana\b/gi, 'ke mana');

    return text;
  }

  /**
   * Normalisasi spasi dan jarak antar-paragraf agar rapi, padat, dan best-practices:
   * 1. Melindungi tabel Markdown (| ... | dan | --- |) agar tidak pecah/hancur
   * 2. Melindungi blok diagram / chart / visual slot (:::chart, :::metric, <!-- VISUAL_SLOT -->)
   * 3. Menghilangkan spasi ganda dan trailing whitespace pada baris teks biasa
   * 4. Merapikan tanda baca pada kalimat narasi tanpa merusak format tabel
   * 5. MENGHILANGKAN garis pembatas mandiri (---, ***, ___, ====) di antara bab atau paragraf (Master Editorial Rules)
   * 6. Membatasi penumpukan baris kosong berlebih (maksimal 2 newline berturut-turut \n\n)
   */
  public static normalizeFormattingSpaces(rawText: string): string {
    if (!rawText || typeof rawText !== 'string') return '';

    const lines = rawText.split(/\r?\n/);
    const processedLines: string[] = [];

    let inSpecialBlock = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      let line = lines[i];
      const trimmed = line.trim();

      // Cek apakah ini blok chart / metric
      if (trimmed.startsWith(':::')) {
        inSpecialBlock = !inSpecialBlock;
        processedLines.push(line.trimEnd());
        continue;
      }
      if (inSpecialBlock) {
        processedLines.push(line.trimEnd());
        continue;
      }

      // Lindungi baris tabel Markdown (yang diawali atau memuat karakter '|')
      const isTableRow = trimmed.startsWith('|') || (trimmed.endsWith('|') && trimmed.includes('|'));
      if (isTableRow) {
        // Baris tabel: HINDARI regex tanda baca & penggantian '---' yang merusak delimiter kolom
        processedLines.push(trimmed);
        continue;
      }

      // Lindungi penanda visual slot HTML comment
      if (trimmed.startsWith('<!--') && trimmed.endsWith('-->')) {
        processedLines.push(trimmed);
        continue;
      }

      // Cek apakah baris ini adalah horizontal rule / garis pemisah mandiri (---, ***, ___, ====, dsb.)
      // Master Editorial Rules: buang garis pemisah ini agar transisi bab/paragraf mengalir alami
      if (/^[ \t]*[-*_=]{3,}[ \t]*$/.test(line)) {
        continue; // Buang garis pemisah horizontal
      }

      // Normalisasi teks narasi / paragraf biasa:
      line = line.trimEnd();

      // Satukan spasi horizontal berlebih di dalam baris
      line = line.replace(/([^\t ])[ \t]{2,}([^\t ])/g, '$1 $2');

      // Hapus spasi sebelum tanda baca
      line = line.replace(/\s+([,.:;?!])/g, '$1');

      // Pastikan ada tepat 1 spasi setelah tanda baca jika diikuti huruf/kata
      line = line.replace(/([,;?!])([a-zA-Z])/g, '$1 $2');
      line = line.replace(/\.([a-zA-Z]{2,})/g, '. $1');

      // Rapikan spasi di dalam tanda kurung
      line = line.replace(/\(\s+/g, '(').replace(/\s+\)/g, ')');

      processedLines.push(line);
    }

    // Gabungkan kembali
    let result = processedLines.join('\n');

    // Rampingkan baris kosong berlebih: maksimal 2 newline (\n\n) agar tidak ada kekosongan masif
    result = result.replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n');

    return result.trim();
  }

  /**
   * Eksekusi End-to-End Two-Stage Pipeline
   */
  public static async execute(params: GenerateArticleParams): Promise<GeneratedArticleResult> {
    const telemetry = AiTelemetryService.startTrace({
      operation: 'generate-two-stage-modular-article',
      model: 'gpt-4o',
      tenantId: params.tenantId,
      metadata: {
        topic: params.topic,
        targetLength: params.targetLength,
        writingStyle: params.writingStyle,
      },
    });

    try {
      const lengthPlan = this.resolveLengthPlan(params.targetLength);

      // 1. TAHAP 1: Generate Outline
      if (params.onProgress) {
        await params.onProgress('Merumuskan Cetak Biru & Struktur Bab Kebijakan...', 25);
      }
      const { outline, tokensUsed: outlineTokens } = await this.generateOutline(params, lengthPlan);

      let totalTokens = outlineTokens;
      const chapterContents: string[] = [];
      let runningSummary = '';

      // 2. TAHAP 2: Ekspansi Bab Berantai (Staggered Context Chaining)
      const totalChapters = outline.chapters.length;
      for (let idx = 0; idx < totalChapters; idx++) {
        const chapter = outline.chapters[idx];
        const progressPercent = 30 + Math.round(((idx + 1) / totalChapters) * 35); // 30% -> 65%

        if (params.onProgress) {
          await params.onProgress(
            `Menguraikan Naskah Substantif ${chapter.chapterNumber}: "${chapter.title}" (${idx + 1}/${totalChapters})...`,
            progressPercent
          );
        }

        const chapterResult = await this.generateChapter(
          chapter,
          outline.title,
          params,
          runningSummary
        );

        chapterContents.push(chapterResult.chapterMarkdown);
        runningSummary = chapterResult.chapterSummary;
        totalTokens += chapterResult.tokensUsed;
      }

      // Gabungkan keseluruhan naskah secara bersih dan padat
      const rawCombined = [
        `# ${outline.title}`,
        `> **Ringkasan Eksekutif:** ${outline.excerpt}`,
        '---',
        ...chapterContents,
      ].join('\n\n');

      const fullMarkdown = TwoStageArticleEngine.normalizeFormattingSpaces(rawCombined);

      const wordCount = fullMarkdown.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;

      if (params.onProgress) {
        await params.onProgress('Menyelesaikan Konsolidasi Naskah & Pemeriksaan Integritas Data...', 68);
      }

      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: Math.round(totalTokens * 0.4),
        outputTokens: Math.round(totalTokens * 0.6),
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        outputPreview: {
          title: outline.title,
          wordCount,
          chaptersGenerated: totalChapters,
        },
      });

      return {
        title: outline.title,
        excerpt: outline.excerpt,
        contentMarkdown: fullMarkdown,
        wordCount,
        totalTokensUsed: totalTokens,
        canonicalTraceId: telemetry.canonicalTraceId,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      await telemetry.endTrace({
        inputTokens: 0,
        outputTokens: 0,
        status: 'FAILED',
        errorMessage: error.message,
      });
      throw new Error(`[TwoStageArticleEngineError] Gagal menyusun naskah artikel: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }
}

export class CreativePosterCompositor {
  private static fontCache: Array<{
    name: string;
    data: Buffer;
    weight: 400 | 600 | 700 | 800;
    style: 'normal';
  }> | null = null;

  /**
   * Menemukan direktori assets/fonts secara dinamis
   */
  private static async findFontsDir(): Promise<string> {
    const candidates = [
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'assets/fonts'),
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), '../assets/fonts'),
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), '../../assets/fonts'),
      path.resolve(process.cwd(), '../../../assets/fonts'),
      '/home/pc/projects/polaris-platform/assets/fonts',
    ];

    for (const dir of candidates) {
      try {
        await fs.access(dir);
        return dir;
      } catch {
        // Coba kandidat berikutnya
      }
    }
    return path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'assets/fonts');
  }

  /**
   * Memuat file binary font lokal sekali ke memori (Singleton)
   * Menghindari bug fatal librsvg yang gagal memuat @import Google Fonts di Docker Linux
   */
  private static async getFonts() {
    if (this.fontCache) return this.fontCache;

    const fontsDir = await this.findFontsDir();

    const [antonData, bebasData, jakartaData, dejavuData, dejavuBoldData] = await Promise.all([
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'Anton-Regular.ttf')),
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'BebasNeue-Regular.ttf')),
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold.ttf')),
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'DejaVuSerif.ttf')).catch(() => fs.readFile('/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSerif.ttf')),
      fs.readFile(path.join(fontsDir, 'DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf')).catch(() => fs.readFile('/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf')),
    ]);

    this.fontCache = [
      { name: 'Anton', data: antonData, weight: 400, style: 'normal' },
      { name: 'Bebas Neue', data: bebasData, weight: 400, style: 'normal' },
      // Daftarkan Plus Jakarta Sans di berbagai weight agar Satori tidak pernah gagal mencocokkan
      { name: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', data: jakartaData, weight: 400, style: 'normal' },
      { name: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', data: jakartaData, weight: 600, style: 'normal' },
      { name: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', data: jakartaData, weight: 700, style: 'normal' },
      // Daftarkan DejaVu Serif untuk typography koran/broadsheet
      { name: 'DejaVu Serif', data: dejavuData, weight: 400, style: 'normal' },
      { name: 'DejaVu Serif', data: dejavuBoldData, weight: 700, style: 'normal' },
    ];

    return this.fontCache;
  }

  /**
   * Mengharmonisasikan foto cutout PNG Anggota Dewan agar menyatu dengan tekstur poster
   */
  private static async harmonizeSubjectPhoto(
    photoBuffer: Buffer,
    archetype: string,
    targetWidth: number,
    targetHeight: number
  ): Promise<Buffer> {
    let pipeline = sharp(photoBuffer).resize({
      width: targetWidth,
      height: targetHeight,
      fit: 'contain',
      background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },
    });

    if (archetype === 'STREET_JOURNALISM') {
      // Ubah ke High-Contrast B&W seperti foto Bapak PKL / Petugas Sampah di kolase
      pipeline = pipeline.grayscale().linear(1.28, -18).sharpen();
    }

    return await pipeline.png().toBuffer();
  }

                            type: 'span',
                            props: { children: word.toUpperCase() },
                          })),
                        },
                      },
                      {

                        props: {
                          style: {
                            fontSize: '12.5px',
                            color: '#18181B',
                            lineHeight: 1.4,
                            textAlign: 'justify',
                          },
                          children: centerStory.body.toUpperCase(),
                        },
                      },
                    ],
                  },
                },

                // --- KOLOM KANAN (Ruang Kosong di Atas untuk Cutout + Teks di Tengah) ---
                {
                  type: 'div',
                  props: {
                    style: {
                      flex: 1,
                      display: 'flex',
                      flexDirection: 'column',
                      paddingTop: '275px', // Area y: 0-275px dibiarkan transparan agar foto Cutout Kanan Atas terlihat utuh!
                      gap: '8px',

                    children: [

                        type: 'div',

                      {

                        props: {
